   private void generateEnd(World world, Random random, int i, int j) {
   }

   private Block soilBlock(int xx, int zz, World world) {
      return (Block)(world.func_72807_a(xx, zz).field_76752_A != null ? world.func_72807_a(xx, zz).field_76752_A : Blocks.field_150349_c);
   }

   private Block fillerBlock(int xx, int zz, World world) {
      return world.func_72807_a(xx, zz).field_76753_B != null ? world.func_72807_a(xx, zz).field_76753_B : Blocks.field_150346_d;
   }

   private boolean validGroundMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151577_b
         || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151578_c
         || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151595_p;
   }

   private boolean validGroundOrLMireMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.MireBlock
         || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.StableLiquidMireBlock
         || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151577_b
         || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151578_c
         || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151595_p;
   }

   private boolean validCaveMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151576_e
         || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151577_b
         || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151578_c
         || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151595_p;
   }

   private boolean validSnowMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151597_y || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151596_z;
   }

   private boolean validFallingMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151595_p || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o().func_76224_d();
   }

   private boolean validSandMaterial(boolean sndO, int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return sndO
         ? world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151595_p
         : world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151577_b
            || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151578_c
            || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151595_p;
   }

   private boolean validClayMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151571_B;
   }

   private boolean validWaterMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h;
   }

   private boolean validLavaMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151587_i;
   }

   private boolean validLiqMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o().func_76224_d();
   }

   private boolean validCSMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) != MFQM.AshBlock && world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151595_p;
   }

   private boolean validBloodMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == Blocks.field_150424_aL;
   }

   private boolean validVoreMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.MeatBlock && world.func_72805_g(x, y, z) == 0 || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.FleshBlock;
   }

   private boolean validTarMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.TarBlock || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o().func_76220_a();
   }

   private boolean validLarvMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.LarvaeBlock || this.validGroundMaterial(x, y, z, world);
   }

   private boolean validSolidMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o().func_76220_a();
   }

   private boolean validMorassMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o().func_76220_a() || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.MorassBlock;
   }

   private boolean validSlimeMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o().func_76220_a() || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.SlimeBlock;
   }

   private boolean validHoneycombMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.HoneycombBlock;
   }

   private boolean validWebbingMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o().func_76220_a() || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.DenseWebBlock;
   }

   private boolean validSlurryMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o().func_76220_a() || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.SlurryBlock;
   }

   private boolean validWebMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()
         || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.DenseWebBlock
         || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == Blocks.field_150321_G;
   }

   private boolean validGroundClayMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.HClayBlock
         || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151577_b
         || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151578_c
         || world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151595_p;
   }

   private boolean validSoftQuicksandMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      if (this.validGroundMaterial(x, y, z, world)) {
         return true;
      } else {
         return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o().func_76220_a() ? true : world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock;
      }
   }

   private boolean validPlantMaterial(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      if (world.func_147439_a(x, y, z) == MFQM.TentBlock) {
         return false;
      } else if (world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151570_A) {
         return true;
      } else if (world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151582_l) {
         return true;
      } else {
         return world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151585_k
            ? true
            : world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151584_j;
      }
   }

   private boolean genIsFlatGroundSlime(int x, int y, int z, int r, World world) {
      r /= 2;
      return (this.validGroundMaterial(x + r, y - 1, z + r, world) || this.validSlimeMaterial(x + r, y - 1, z + r, world))
         && (this.validGroundMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z + r, world) || this.validSlimeMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z + r, world))
         && (this.validGroundMaterial(x + r, y - 1, z - r, world) || this.validSlimeMaterial(x + r, y - 1, z - r, world))
         && (this.validGroundMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z - r, world) || this.validSlimeMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z - r, world))
         && (this.validGroundMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z, world) || this.validSlimeMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z, world))
         && (this.validGroundMaterial(x, y - 1, z - r, world) || this.validSlimeMaterial(x, y - 1, z - r, world))
         && (this.validGroundMaterial(x + r, y - 1, z, world) || this.validSlimeMaterial(x + r, y - 1, z, world))
         && (this.validGroundMaterial(x, y - 1, z + r, world) || this.validSlimeMaterial(x, y - 1, z + r, world));
   }

   private boolean genIsFlatSoftQuicksand(int x, int y, int z, int r, World world) {
      r /= 2;
      return this.validSoftQuicksandMaterial(x + r, y - 1, z + r, world)
         && this.validSoftQuicksandMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z + r, world)
         && this.validSoftQuicksandMaterial(x + r, y - 1, z - r, world)
         && this.validSoftQuicksandMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z - r, world)
         && this.validSoftQuicksandMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z, world)
         && this.validSoftQuicksandMaterial(x, y - 1, z - r, world)
         && this.validSoftQuicksandMaterial(x + r, y - 1, z, world)
         && this.validSoftQuicksandMaterial(x, y - 1, z + r, world);
   }

   private boolean genIsFlat(int x, int y, int z, int r, World world) {
      r /= 2;
      return this.validGroundMaterial(x + r, y - 1, z + r, world)
         && this.validGroundMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z + r, world)
         && this.validGroundMaterial(x + r, y - 1, z - r, world)
         && this.validGroundMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z - r, world)
         && this.validGroundMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z, world)
         && this.validGroundMaterial(x, y - 1, z - r, world)
         && this.validGroundMaterial(x + r, y - 1, z, world)
         && this.validGroundMaterial(x, y - 1, z + r, world);
   }

   private boolean genIsFlatHoneycomb(int x, int y, int z, int r, World world) {
      r /= 2;
      return this.validHoneycombMaterial(x + r, y - 1, z + r, world)
         && this.validHoneycombMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z + r, world)
         && this.validHoneycombMaterial(x + r, y - 1, z - r, world)
         && this.validHoneycombMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z - r, world)
         && this.validHoneycombMaterial(x - r, y - 1, z, world)

   private boolean genIsBrickNear(int x, int y, int z, int r, World world) {
      r /= 2;
      if (world.func_147439_a(x + r, y, z + r) == Blocks.field_150417_aV) {
         return true;
      } else if (world.func_147439_a(x - r, y, z + r) == Blocks.field_150417_aV) {
         return true;
      } else if (world.func_147439_a(x + r, y, z - r) == Blocks.field_150417_aV) {
         return true;
      } else if (world.func_147439_a(x - r, y, z - r) == Blocks.field_150417_aV) {
         return true;
      } else if (world.func_147439_a(x - r, y, z) == Blocks.field_150417_aV) {
         return true;
      } else if (world.func_147439_a(x, y, z - r) == Blocks.field_150417_aV) {
         return true;
      } else {
         return world.func_147439_a(x + r, y, z) == Blocks.field_150417_aV ? true : world.func_147439_a(x, y, z + r) == Blocks.field_150417_aV;
      }
   }

   private boolean genIsUnderWaterFlat(int x, int y, int z, int r, World world) {
      r /= 2;
      return this.validWaterMaterial(x + r, y, z + r, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x + r + 2, y, z + r + 2, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x - r, y, z + r, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x - r - 2, y, z + r + 2, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x + r, y, z - r, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x + r + 2, y, z - r - 2, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x - r, y, z - r, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x - r - 2, y, z - r - 2, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x - r, y, z, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x - r - 2, y, z, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x, y, z - r, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x, y, z - r - 2, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x + r, y, z, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x + r + 2, y, z, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x, y, z + r, world)
         && this.validWaterMaterial(x, y, z + r + 2, world);
   }

   public static boolean isAboveFreeSpace(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      int re = 2 + world.field_73012_v.nextInt(3);
      y++;

      for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
         if (world.func_147439_a(x, y + i, z).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
            return false;
         }
      }

      return true;
   }

   public static boolean isAboveSky(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      int re = 15;
      y++;

      for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
         if (world.func_147439_a(x, y + i, z).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
            return false;
         }
      }

      return true;
   }

   private boolean isAboveEmpty(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      int re = 4 + world.field_73012_v.nextInt(3);
      y++;

      for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
         if (!world.func_147437_c(x, y + i, z)) {
            return false;
         }
      }

      return true;
   }

   private boolean isAbovePlant(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      int re = 4 + world.field_73012_v.nextInt(3);
      y++;

      for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
         if (world.func_147439_a(x, y + i, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151584_j) {
            return true;
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private boolean isAboveSolid(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      int re = 10 + world.field_73012_v.nextInt(3);
      y++;

      for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
         if (this.validCaveMaterial(x, y + i, z, world)) {
            return true;
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private boolean isNearAboveCeil(int x, int y, int z, World world, int rad) {
      int re = 18;
      int groundCount = 0;
      if (!this.isAboveEmpty(x, y + 2, z, world)) {
         for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
            float rg = world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 360.0F;
            float imp = rad / 6 + world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 2.0F;
            rg = (float)Math.toRadians(rg);
            int xx = x + (int)Math.floor(Math.sin(rg) * imp);
            int zz = z + (int)Math.floor(Math.cos(rg) * imp);
            if (!this.isAboveEmpty(xx, y + 2, zz, world)) {
               groundCount++;
            }

            if (groundCount > 5) {
               return true;
            }
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private boolean isNearAbovePlant(int x, int y, int z, World world, int rad) {
      int re = 18;
      int groundCount = 0;
      if (!this.isAboveEmpty(x, y + 2, z, world)) {
         for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
            float rg = world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 360.0F;
            float imp = rad / 6 + world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 2.0F;
            rg = (float)Math.toRadians(rg);
            int xx = x + (int)Math.floor(Math.sin(rg) * imp);
            int zz = z + (int)Math.floor(Math.cos(rg) * imp);
            if (this.isAbovePlant(xx, y + 2, zz, world)) {
               groundCount++;
            }

            if (groundCount > 2) {
               return true;
            }
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private boolean isInCave(int x, int y, int z, World world, int rad) {
      int re = 18;
      int groundCount = 0;
      if (this.isAboveSolid(x, y + 2, z, world)) {
         for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
            float rg = world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 360.0F;
            float imp = rad / 6 + world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 2.0F;
            rg = (float)Math.toRadians(rg);
            int xx = x + (int)Math.floor(Math.sin(rg) * imp);
            int zz = z + (int)Math.floor(Math.cos(rg) * imp);
            if (this.isAboveSolid(xx, y + 2, zz, world)) {
               groundCount++;
            }

            if (groundCount > 5) {
               return true;
            }
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private boolean isWaterNear(int x, int y, int z, int rad, World world) {
      int re = 18;
      int waterCount = 0;

      for (int l = 0; l < 1; l++) {
         for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
            float rg = world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 360.0F;
            float imp = rad / 4 + world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 2.0F;
            rg = (float)Math.toRadians(rg);
            int xx = x + (int)Math.floor(Math.sin(rg) * imp);
            int zz = z + (int)Math.floor(Math.cos(rg) * imp);
            if (this.validWaterMaterial(xx, y - l, zz, world) && !world.func_147439_a(x, y - l + 1, z).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
               waterCount++;
            }

            if (waterCount > 9) {
               return true;
            }
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private boolean isGroundNear(int x, int y, int z, int rad, World world) {
      int re = 18;
      int groundCount = 0;

      for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
         float rg = world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 360.0F;
         float imp = rad / 4 + world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 2.0F;
         rg = (float)Math.toRadians(rg);
         int xx = x + (int)Math.floor(Math.sin(rg) * imp);
         int zz = z + (int)Math.floor(Math.cos(rg) * imp);
         if (this.validGroundMaterial(xx, y, zz, world)) {
            groundCount++;
         }

         if (groundCount > 5) {
            return true;
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private boolean isGroundQS(int x, int y, int z, int rad, World world, Block qsBlock) {
      int re = 18;
      int groundCount = 0;

      for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
         float rg = world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 360.0F;
         float imp = rad / 4 + world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 2.0F;
         rg = (float)Math.toRadians(rg);
         int xx = x + (int)Math.floor(Math.sin(rg) * imp);
         int zz = z + (int)Math.floor(Math.cos(rg) * imp);
         if (this.validGroundMaterial(xx, y, zz, world) || world.func_147439_a(xx, y, zz) == qsBlock) {
            groundCount++;
         }

         if (groundCount > 5) {
            return true;
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private boolean isGroundMossNear(int x, int y, int z, int rad, World world) {
      int re = 18;
      int groundCount = 0;

      for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
         float rg = world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 360.0F;
         float imp = rad / 4 + world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 2.0F;
         rg = (float)Math.toRadians(rg);
         int xx = x + (int)Math.floor(Math.sin(rg) * imp);
         int zz = z + (int)Math.floor(Math.cos(rg) * imp);
         if (this.validGroundMaterial(xx, y, zz, world)
            || world.func_147439_a(xx, y + 1, zz) == MFQM.MossBlock
            || world.func_147439_a(xx, y, zz) == MFQM.BogBlock) {
            groundCount++;
         }

         if (groundCount > 5) {
            return true;
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private boolean isItLake(int x, int y, int z, int rad, World world) {
      int re = 8;
      int groundCount = 0;

      for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
         float rg = i * 45.0F;
         float imp = rad / 2 + world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 2.0F;
         rg = (float)Math.toRadians(rg);
         int xx = x + (int)Math.floor(Math.sin(rg) * imp);
         int zz = z + (int)Math.floor(Math.cos(rg) * imp);
         if (this.validGroundMaterial(xx, y, zz, world)) {
            groundCount++;
         }

         if (groundCount > 7) {
            return true;
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private boolean isItLake2(int x, int y, int z, int rad, World world, boolean solid) {
      int re = 8;
      int groundCount = 0;

      for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
         float rg = i * 45.0F;
         float imp = rad / 2 + world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 2.0F;
         rg = (float)Math.toRadians(rg);
         int xx = x + (int)Math.floor(Math.sin(rg) * imp);
         int zz = z + (int)Math.floor(Math.cos(rg) * imp);
         if (this.validGroundMaterial(xx, y, zz, world) || solid && this.validSolidMaterial(xx, y, zz, world)) {
            groundCount++;
         }

         if (groundCount > 7) {
            return true;
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private boolean isGroundBogNear(int x, int y, int z, int rad, World world) {
      int re = 18;
      int groundCount = 0;

      for (int i = 0; i < re; i++) {
         float rg = world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 360.0F;
         float imp = rad / 4 + world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 2.0F;
         rg = (float)Math.toRadians(rg);
         int xx = x + (int)Math.floor(Math.sin(rg) * imp);
         int zz = z + (int)Math.floor(Math.cos(rg) * imp);
         if (this.validGroundMaterial(xx, y, zz, world) || world.func_147439_a(xx, y, zz) == MFQM.BogBlock) {
            groundCount++;
         }

         if (groundCount > 5) {
            return true;
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private boolean isWoodNear(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      boolean liquid = false;
      if (world.func_147439_a(x, y, z).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
         liquid = true;
      }

      if (world.func_147439_a(x, y + 1, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d) {
         if (world.func_147439_a(x, y + 2, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d) {
            if (!liquid) {
               return true;
            }
         } else {
            world.func_147468_f(x, y + 1, z);
         }
      }

      if (world.func_147439_a(x + 1, y + 1, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d) {
         if (world.func_147439_a(x + 1, y + 2, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d) {
            if (!liquid) {
               return true;
            }
         } else {
            world.func_147468_f(x + 1, y + 1, z);
         }
      }

      if (world.func_147439_a(x - 1, y + 1, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d) {
         if (world.func_147439_a(x - 1, y + 2, z).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d) {
            if (!liquid) {
               return true;
            }
         } else {
            world.func_147468_f(x - 1, y + 1, z);
         }
      }

      if (world.func_147439_a(x, y + 1, z + 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d) {
         if (world.func_147439_a(x, y + 2, z + 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d) {
            if (!liquid) {
               return true;
            }
         } else {
            world.func_147468_f(x, y + 1, z + 1);
         }
      }

      if (world.func_147439_a(x, y + 1, z - 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d) {
         if (world.func_147439_a(x, y + 2, z - 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d) {
            if (!liquid) {
               return true;
            }
         } else {
            world.func_147468_f(x, y + 1, z - 1);
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private boolean validVoreDown(int x, int y, int z, World world, int count) {
      for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
         if (!this.validVoreMaterial(x, y - i, z, world)) {
            return false;
         }
      }

      return true;
   }

   private boolean isVorePlace(int x, int y, int z, World world) {
      int deep = 3;
      return this.validVoreDown(x, y, z, world, deep)
         && this.validVoreDown(x - 1, y, z - 1, world, deep)
         && this.validVoreDown(x + 1, y, z - 1, world, deep)
         && this.validVoreDown(x + 1, y, z + 1, world, deep)
         && this.validVoreDown(x - 1, y, z + 1, world, deep)
         && this.validVoreDown(x + 1, y, z, world, deep)
         && this.validVoreDown(x - 1, y, z, world, deep)
         && this.validVoreDown(x, y, z - 1, world, deep)
         && this.validVoreDown(x, y, z + 1, world, deep);
   }

   private boolean IsDifficultSwamp(int ui) {
      return ui == MFQM.SwampBiomes[9] || ui == MFQM.SwampBiomes[10];
   }

   private boolean IsNormalSwamp(int ui) {
      return ui == MFQM.SwampBiomes[2] || ui == MFQM.SwampBiomes[0] || ui == MFQM.SwampBiomes[3] || ui == MFQM.SwampBiomes[11];
   }

   private boolean IsEasySwamp(int ui) {
      return ui == MFQM.SwampBiomes[12];
   }

   private int getSwampDifficult(int ui) {
      if (this.T_BoP) {
         if (this.IsEasySwamp(ui)) {
            return 3;
         } else if (this.IsNormalSwamp(ui)) {
            return 2;
         } else {
            return this.IsDifficultSwamp(ui) ? 1 : 0;
         }
      } else {
         return 0;
      }
   }

   private void generateClearWater(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = 1.0;
      double raz = 1.0;

      for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
         for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0));
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)
                  && this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world)) {
                  world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateBrownClay(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                  }

                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.BrownClayBlock, random.nextInt(4), 2);
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx + 1, posy, posz, MFQM.BrownClayBlock, random.nextInt(4), 2);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx - 1, posy, posz, MFQM.BrownClayBlock, random.nextInt(4), 2);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz + 1, MFQM.BrownClayBlock, random.nextInt(4), 2);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz - 1, MFQM.BrownClayBlock, random.nextInt(4), 2);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      Block sblock = this.soilBlock(xx, zz, world);
      Block fblock = this.fillerBlock(xx, zz, world);

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.BrownClayBlock
                  && !this.validGroundMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world)
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.BrownClayBlock) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, fblock);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)

                     world.func_147465_d(posx + 1, posy, posz, MFQM.BrownClayBlock, random.nextInt(4) + 4, 2);

                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz + 1, MFQM.BrownClayBlock, random.nextInt(4) + 4, 2);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz - 1, MFQM.BrownClayBlock, random.nextInt(4) + 4, 2);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.BrownClayBlock
                  && !this.validGroundMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world)
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.BrownClayBlock) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateMire(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, Material BlockMaterialToReplace, Block MireBlockId) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                  && (BlockMaterialToReplace == Material.field_151568_F || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == BlockMaterialToReplace)) {
                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MireBlockId);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MireBlockId
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SlurryBlock
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MireBlockId) {
                  if (world.field_73011_w.field_76574_g == -1) {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150424_aL);
                  } else {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateMireM(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, Material BlockMaterialToReplace, Block MireBlockId, int Meta) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                  && (BlockMaterialToReplace == Material.field_151568_F || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == BlockMaterialToReplace)) {
                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world) && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149716_u()) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world) && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz).func_149716_u()) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world) && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 3, posz).func_149716_u()) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d
                     && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149716_u()) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MireBlockId, Meta, 0);
                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 1, posz, MireBlockId, Meta, 0);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MireBlockId && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MireBlockId) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateMireO(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, Material BlockMaterialToReplace, Block MireBlockId) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      Block aBlock = null;
      int aMeta = 0;
      boolean aDetBlock = false;
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && (BlockMaterialToReplace == Material.field_151568_F || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == BlockMaterialToReplace)) {
                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                  }

                  if (!aDetBlock) {
                     aBlock = world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz);
                     if (aBlock != null
                        && aBlock.func_149688_o().func_76220_a()
                        && aBlock.func_149662_c()
                        && !aBlock.func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                        && !aBlock.func_149716_u()) {
                        aMeta = world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz);
                        aDetBlock = true;
                     }
                  }

                  if (j == 0
                     && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) != MireBlockId
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MireBlockId);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (aDetBlock) {
         for (int i = -1; i <= rad + 1; i++) {
            for (int j = -1; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
               for (int ex = -1; ex <= rad + 1; ex++) {
                  int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
                  int posy = yy - j;
                  int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MireBlockId
                     && !this.validGroundMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world)
                     && (
                        world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) == MireBlockId
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) == MireBlockId
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MireBlockId
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) == MireBlockId
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) == MireBlockId
                     )) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, aBlock, aMeta, 0);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateMud(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      int difq = 0;
      if (random.nextInt(4) == 0) {
         difq++;
      }

      if (random.nextInt(5) == 0) {
         difq++;
      }

      if (random.nextInt(6) == 0) {
         difq++;
      }

      if (random.nextInt(7) == 0) {
         difq++;
      }

      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy + j - rad / 2;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               double disQ = rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0);
               if (dis < disQ
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151578_c
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151595_p
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151577_b
                  )) {
                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                  }

                  int metm = 0;
                  int metq = 0;
                  if (MFQM.GenDeep) {
                     if (dis > disQ * 2.0 / 3.0) {
                        metq = 0;
                     }

                     if (dis > disQ / 3.0) {
                        metq = Math.max((int)(Math.floor(difq / 2.0) - Math.floor((rad / 2 - j) / 8.0)), 0);
                     }

                     if (dis <= disQ / 3.0) {
                        metq = Math.max(Math.min((int)(difq - Math.floor((rad / 2 - j) / 8.0)), 3), 0);
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.MudBlock) {
                        metm = world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz);
                     }
                  }

                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MudBlock, Math.max(metm, metq), 2);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateForcedMud(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, int depth) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      int difq = 0;
      if (depth < 1) {
         if (random.nextInt(4) == 0) {
            difq++;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(5) == 0) {
            difq++;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(6) == 0) {
            difq++;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(7) == 0) {
            difq++;
         }
      } else {
         difq = depth;
      }

      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy + j - rad / 2;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               double disQ = rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0);
               if (MFQM.EUD
                  && world.field_73011_w.field_76574_g == MFQM.EUDDim
                  && j == rad / 2
                  && this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)
                  && !this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                  world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
               }

               if (dis < disQ && this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world) && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                  }

                  int metm = 0;
                  int metq = 0;
                  if (MFQM.GenDeep) {
                     if (depth == 5) {
                        metq = 3;
                        metm = 3;
                     } else {
                        if (dis > disQ * 2.0 / 3.0) {
                           metq = 0;
                        }

                        if (dis > disQ / 3.0) {
                           metq = Math.max((int)(Math.floor(difq / 2.0) - Math.floor((rad / 2 - j) / 8.0)), 0);
                        }

                        if (dis <= disQ / 3.0) {
                           metq = Math.max(Math.min((int)(difq - Math.floor((rad / 2 - j) / 8.0)), 3), 0);
                        }

                        if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.MudBlock) {
                           metm = world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz);
                        }
                     }
                  }

                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MudBlock, Math.max(metm, metq), 2);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateFlatMud(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      int difq = 0;
      if (random.nextInt(2) == 0) {
         difq++;
      }

      if (random.nextInt(3) == 0) {
         difq++;
      }

      if (random.nextInt(4) == 0) {
         difq++;
      }

      if (random.nextInt(5) == 0) {
         difq++;
      }

      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               double disQ = rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0);
               if (dis < disQ
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.StableLiquidMireBlock
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.LiquidMireBlock
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.MireBlock
                  && (
                     !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149662_c()
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == Blocks.field_150355_j
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151578_c
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151595_p
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151577_b
                  )) {
                  int metm = 0;
                  int metq = 0;
                  if (MFQM.GenDeep) {
                     if (dis > disQ * 2.0 / 3.0) {
                        metq = 0;
                     }

                     if (dis > disQ / 3.0) {
                        metq = Math.max((int)(Math.floor(difq / 2.0) - Math.floor((rad / 2 - j) / 4.0)), 0);
                     }

                     if (dis <= disQ / 3.0) {
                        metq = Math.max(Math.min((int)(difq - Math.floor((rad / 2 - j) / 4.0)), 3), 0);
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.MudBlock) {
                        metm = world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz);
                     }
                  }

                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MudBlock, Math.max(metm, metq), 2);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateMud2(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && (this.validBloodMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world) || this.validVoreMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world))) {
                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                  }

                  if (random.nextInt(4) == 0) {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy + 1, posz, MFQM.MudBlock);
                  }

                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MudBlock);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.MudBlock);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, MFQM.MudBlock);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 3, posz, MFQM.MudBlock);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 4, posz, MFQM.MudBlock);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 5, posz, MFQM.MudBlock);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateMire2(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, Material BlockMaterialToReplace, Block MireBlockId) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.MeatBlock || world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz) == 0)
                  && (BlockMaterialToReplace == Material.field_151568_F || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == BlockMaterialToReplace)) {
                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MireBlockId);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MireBlockId);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MireBlockId
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) == MireBlockId
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) == MireBlockId
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MireBlockId
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) == MireBlockId
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) == MireBlockId
                  )) {
                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 0, 2);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateMossQS(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, Block qsblock) {
      Block sblock = this.soilBlock(xx, zz, world);
      Block fblock = this.fillerBlock(xx, zz, world);
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
         for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) {
                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) != qsblock && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149662_c()) {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy + 1, posz, MFQM.MossBlock);
                  }

                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, qsblock);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, qsblock);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != qsblock
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) == qsblock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) == qsblock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == qsblock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) == qsblock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) == qsblock
                  )) {
                  if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149662_c()) {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, sblock);
                  } else {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, fblock);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateMireOD(
      int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, Material BlockMaterialToReplace, Block MireBlockId, int MireBlockMeta
   ) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      Block aBlock = null;
      int aMeta = 0;
      boolean aDetBlock = false;
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.TarBlock
                  && (BlockMaterialToReplace == Material.field_151568_F || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == BlockMaterialToReplace)) {
                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (!aDetBlock) {
                     aBlock = world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz);
                     if (aBlock != null
                        && aBlock.func_149688_o().func_76220_a()
                        && aBlock.func_149662_c()
                        && !aBlock.func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                        && !aBlock.func_149716_u()) {
                        aMeta = world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz);
                        aDetBlock = true;
                     }
                  }

                  if (j == 0) {
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                        if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d) {
                           world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz), world.func_72805_g(posx, posy + 1, posz), 2);
                        } else {
                           world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
                        }
                     }

                     int re = world.field_73012_v.nextInt(4);
                     switch (re) {
                        case 0:
                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx - 1, posy, posz);
                           }
                           break;
                        case 1:
                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx + 1, posy, posz);
                           }
                           break;
                        case 2:
                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz - 1);
                           }
                           break;
                        case 3:
                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {

   private void generateMireD(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, Material BlockMaterialToReplace, Block MireBlockId, boolean forced) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);

            return;
         }
      } else if (random.nextInt(1) != 0) {
         this.generateMire(xx, yy, zz, rad, world, random, BlockMaterialToReplace, MireBlockId);

         && world.func_147439_a(xx, yy, zz) != Blocks.field_150354_m

               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == Blocks.field_150350_a
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151585_k

   private void generateHoneyNest(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      int acount = rad * 2;

      for (int aNeedToGen = rad / 2; aNeedToGen > 0 && acount > 0; acount--) {

            this.generateHoneycomb(posx, posy, posz, arad, world, random, ahoney);

         this.generateSolidHoney(posx, posy, posz, arad, world, random);
      }

      for (int j = 0; j <= rad * 2; j++) {
         for (int i = 0; i <= rad * 4; i++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad * 4; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad * 2;
               int posy = yy + j - rad;

                  || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.SolidHoneyBlock && world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz) > 0;
               if (isHon) {

                     world.func_147465_d(posx + 1, posy, posz, MFQM.SolidHoneyBlock, 0, 0);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.HoneycombBlock && world.func_72805_g(posx - 1, posy, posz) != 3) {

                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.SolidHoneyBlock, 0, 0);

                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz - 1, MFQM.SolidHoneyBlock, 0, 0);

               }

         }
      }

      int var19 = rad / 8 + 2;

      for (int var17 = rad * 64; var19 > 0 && var17 > 0; var17--) {

         int posy = yy + random.nextInt(rad) - rad / 2;

         if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.HoneycombBlock
            && world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz) != 3

            && world.func_72805_g(posx + 1, posy, posz) != 3

   public void generateHoneyChest(World world, int x, int y, int z, int meta, Random rand) {
      world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, Blocks.field_150486_ae, meta, 2);
      TileEntityChest var12 = (TileEntityChest)world.func_147438_o(x, y, z);
      ItemStack tmpStack = new ItemStack(Items.field_151045_i, 1, 0);

      for (int i = 31999; i > 1; i--) {
         Item tmp = Item.func_150899_d(i);
         if (tmp != null) {
            Item tmp2 = Item.func_150899_d(i - rand.nextInt(i - 1));
            if (tmp2 != null) {
               int tmpMeta;
               int tmpCount;
               if (tmp2.func_77639_j() == 1 && tmp2.func_77645_m()) {
                  tmpMeta = 0;
                  tmpCount = 1;
               } else {
                  tmpMeta = rand.nextInt(Math.max(tmp2.func_77612_l(), 1));
                  int maxCount = Math.max(tmp2.func_77639_j() - 1, 1);
                  int partCount = 1;
                  tmpCount = 1;
                  if (maxCount > 4) {
                     partCount = Math.max(maxCount / 4, 1);
                     tmpCount += rand.nextInt(partCount);
                     if (rand.nextInt(partCount) == 0) {
                        tmpCount += rand.nextInt(partCount + 1);
                     }

                     if (rand.nextInt(partCount) == 0) {
                        tmpCount += rand.nextInt(partCount + 1);
                     }

                     if (rand.nextInt(partCount) == 0) {
                        tmpCount += rand.nextInt(partCount + 1);
                     }
                  } else {
                     tmpCount = rand.nextInt(maxCount) + 1;
                  }
               }

               tmpStack = new ItemStack(tmp2, tmpCount, tmpMeta);
               break;
            }
         }
      }

      if (var12 != null) {
         var12.func_145976_a("Honey Chest");
         WeightedRandomChestContent[] content = ChestGenHooks.getItems("dungeonChest", rand);
         List<WeightedRandomChestContent> listFromArray = Arrays.asList(content);

         tempList.add(new WeightedRandomChestContent(Items.field_151045_i, 0, 1, 3, 16));
         tempList.add(new WeightedRandomChestContent(Items.field_151043_k, 0, 4, 16, 16));
         tempList.add(new WeightedRandomChestContent(Items.field_151166_bC, 0, 1, 3, 16));

         content = tempList.toArray(tempArray);

         var12.func_70299_a(rand.nextInt(var12.func_70302_i_()), tmpStack);
      }
   }

   private void generateHoneycomb(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, int honey) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      int aType = random.nextInt(3);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;
      int honeyHeight = 0;
      int min_posy = 999;
      double minRad = rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) / (1.5 + random.nextDouble());

      for (int j = 0; j <= rad; j++) {
         for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy + j - rad / 2;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0)) {
                  boolean isHon = world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.HoneyBlock
                     || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.SolidHoneyBlock && world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz) > 0;
                  if (isHon) {
                     honeyHeight = posy;
                  }

                  if (dis < minRad) {
                     if (min_posy > posy) {
                        min_posy = posy;
                     }
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.HoneycombBlock || world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz) != 3) {
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
                     }

                     if (random.nextInt(3) == 0) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.HoneycombBlock, random.nextInt(3), 0);
                     } else {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.HoneycombBlock, aType, 0);
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (honeyHeight < 1 && honey < 10 && random.nextInt(honey) == 0) {
         honeyHeight = min_posy + 1 + random.nextInt(2);
      }

      for (int j = 0; j <= rad; j++) {
         for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy + j - rad / 2;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < minRad) {
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
                  }

                  if (posy <= honeyHeight) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SolidHoneyBlock, 15, 0);
                  } else {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.HoneycombBlock, 3, 0);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateSolidHoney(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;
      boolean water = true;
      if (random.nextInt(3) == 0) {
         water = false;
      }

      for (int j = 0; j <= rad; j++) {
         for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy + j - rad / 2;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0)) {
                  boolean isHon = world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.HoneyBlock
                     || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.SolidHoneyBlock && world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz) > 0;
                  if (isHon) {
                     if (water
                        && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) != MFQM.HoneyBlock
                        && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) != MFQM.SolidHoneyBlock) {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SolidHoneyBlock, 0, 0);
                     }
                  } else if (!water && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.HoneycombBlock && world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz) != 3) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SolidHoneyBlock, 0, 0);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateBlood(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, Material BlockMaterialToReplace) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == BlockMaterialToReplace
                        || BlockMaterialToReplace == Material.field_151568_F && this.validBloodMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world)
                  )) {
                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 0, 0);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateBlood2(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad * 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy + j - rad;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && (
                     this.validBloodMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world)
                        || this.validLiqMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world)
                           && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.AcidBlock
                           && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SlurryBlock
                           && world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz) == 0
                  )) {
                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 0, 2);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateBlood3(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      yy += rad / 4;
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy + j - rad / 2;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.MeatBlock && posy > yy - rad / 4 && dis < rad / 2.0) {
                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 0, 2);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }
