   private void genColumnBlockMeta(int xx, int yy, int zz, World world, int count, Block blockId, int meta) {
      for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
         world.func_147465_d(xx, yy - i, zz, blockId, meta, 0);
      }
   }

   private void preventLiquid(int xx, int yy, int zz, World world, Block blockId, int meta) {
      if (world.func_147439_a(xx + 1, yy, zz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
         world.func_147465_d(xx + 1, yy, zz, blockId, meta, 2);
      }

      if (world.func_147439_a(xx - 1, yy, zz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
         world.func_147465_d(xx - 1, yy, zz, blockId, meta, 2);
      }

      if (world.func_147439_a(xx, yy, zz - 1).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
         world.func_147465_d(xx, yy, zz - 1, blockId, meta, 2);
      }

      if (world.func_147439_a(xx, yy, zz + 1).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
         world.func_147465_d(xx, yy, zz + 1, blockId, meta, 2);
      }
   }

   private void generateSlurry(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      int stumb = 0;
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) {
                  if (j == 0 && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) != MFQM.SlurryBlock && !this.validLiqMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     if (world.func_72805_g(posx, posy + 1, posz) == 10) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 2, posz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 10, 2);
                     }

                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                     this.preventLiquid(posx, posy + 1, posz, world, MFQM.MudBlock, 0);
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz) != MFQM.SlurryBlock && !this.validLiqMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                        if (world.func_72805_g(posx, posy + 2, posz) == 10) {
                           world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 3, posz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 10, 2);
                        }

                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                        this.preventLiquid(posx, posy + 2, posz, world, MFQM.MudBlock, 0);
                        if (random.nextInt(2) == 0
                           && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 3, posz) != MFQM.SlurryBlock
                           && !this.validLiqMaterial(posx, posy + 4, posz, world)) {
                           if (world.func_72805_g(posx, posy + 3, posz) == 10) {
                              world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 4, posz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 10, 2);
                           }

                           world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                           this.preventLiquid(posx, posy + 3, posz, world, MFQM.MudBlock, 0);
                           if (random.nextInt(3) == 0
                              && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 4, posz) != MFQM.SlurryBlock
                              && !this.validLiqMaterial(posx, posy + 5, posz, world)) {
                              if (world.func_72805_g(posx, posy + 4, posz) == 10) {
                                 world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 5, posz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 10, 2);
                              }

                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 4, posz);
                              this.preventLiquid(posx, posy + 4, posz, world, MFQM.MudBlock, 0);
                              if (random.nextInt(4) == 0
                                 && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 5, posz) != MFQM.SlurryBlock
                                 && !this.validLiqMaterial(posx, posy + 6, posz, world)) {
                                 if (world.func_72805_g(posx, posy + 5, posz) == 10) {
                                    world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 6, posz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 10, 2);
                                 }

                                 world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 5, posz);
                                 this.preventLiquid(posx, posy + 5, posz, world, MFQM.MudBlock, 0);
                              }
                           }
                        }
                     }
                  }

                  if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(20) == 0 && j == 0) {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MudBlock);
                  } else {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SlurryBlock);
                  }

                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.SlurryBlock);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, MFQM.MudBlock);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SlurryBlock
                  && !this.validGroundMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world)
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.SlurryBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.SlurryBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.SlurryBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.SlurryBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) == MFQM.SlurryBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) == MFQM.SlurryBlock
                  )) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MudBlock);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void ClearArea3(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= 3; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j - 1;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0));
               if (dis < rad / 2.0) {
                  world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private boolean generateAss(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      int firstBlockYCoord = 0;

      boolean canGen;
      for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord < 16; firstBlockYCoord++) {
         if (this.validVoreMaterial(xx, yy + firstBlockYCoord, zz, world) && this.validVoreMaterial(xx, yy + firstBlockYCoord + 1, zz, world)) {
            canGen = true;
            break;
         }

         if (world.func_147439_a(xx, yy + firstBlockYCoord, zz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
            break;
         }
      }

      if (canGen && firstBlockYCoord > 2) {
         if (random.nextInt(2) == 0) {
            firstBlockYCoord++;
         }

         world.func_147465_d(xx, yy + firstBlockYCoord, zz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 10, 2);

         for (int i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
            world.func_147465_d(xx, yy + firstBlockYCoord + i, zz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 0, 2);
            world.func_147465_d(xx + 1, yy + firstBlockYCoord + i - 1, zz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 0, 2);
            world.func_147465_d(xx - 1, yy + firstBlockYCoord + i - 1, zz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 0, 2);
            world.func_147465_d(xx, yy + firstBlockYCoord + i - 1, zz + 1, MFQM.MeatBlock, 0, 2);
            world.func_147465_d(xx, yy + firstBlockYCoord + i - 1, zz - 1, MFQM.MeatBlock, 0, 2);
         }

         this.generateBlood3(xx, yy + firstBlockYCoord + 1, zz, 12, world, random);
         this.generateBlood3(xx + random.nextInt(9) - 4, yy + firstBlockYCoord + 3, zz + random.nextInt(9) - 4, 7 + random.nextInt(5), world, random);
         this.generateBlood3(xx + random.nextInt(9) - 4, yy + firstBlockYCoord + 3, zz + random.nextInt(9) - 4, 7 + random.nextInt(5), world, random);
         this.generateBlood3(xx + random.nextInt(9) - 4, yy + firstBlockYCoord + 3, zz + random.nextInt(9) - 4, 7 + random.nextInt(5), world, random);
         this.generateBlood3(xx + random.nextInt(11) - 5, yy + firstBlockYCoord + 5, zz + random.nextInt(11) - 5, 10 + random.nextInt(5), world, random);
         this.generateBlood3(xx + random.nextInt(11) - 5, yy + firstBlockYCoord + 5, zz + random.nextInt(11) - 5, 10 + random.nextInt(5), world, random);
         this.generateBlood3(xx + random.nextInt(11) - 5, yy + firstBlockYCoord + 5, zz + random.nextInt(11) - 5, 10 + random.nextInt(5), world, random);
         this.generateBlood3(xx + random.nextInt(11) - 5, yy + firstBlockYCoord + 5, zz + random.nextInt(11) - 5, 10 + random.nextInt(5), world, random);
         this.generateBlood3(xx + random.nextInt(11) - 5, yy + firstBlockYCoord + 5, zz + random.nextInt(11) - 5, 10 + random.nextInt(5), world, random);
         this.generateBlood3(xx + random.nextInt(11) - 5, yy + firstBlockYCoord + 5, zz + random.nextInt(11) - 5, 10 + random.nextInt(5), world, random);
         this.ClearArea3(xx, yy + firstBlockYCoord, zz, rad, world, random);
         return true;
      } else {
         return false;
      }
   }

   private void generateSlime(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      Block aBlock = null;
      int aMeta = 0;
      boolean aDetBlock = false;
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) {
                  if (j == 0
                     && this.validCaveMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)
                     && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)
                     && !isAboveFreeSpace(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                     if (this.validCaveMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)
                        && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)
                        && !isAboveFreeSpace(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                        if (random.nextInt(1) == 0
                           && this.validCaveMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)
                           && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 4, posz, world)
                           && !isAboveFreeSpace(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                           world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                           if (random.nextInt(2) == 0
                              && this.validCaveMaterial(posx, posy + 4, posz, world)
                              && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 5, posz, world)
                              && !isAboveFreeSpace(posx, posy + 4, posz, world)) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 4, posz);
                              if (random.nextInt(3) == 0
                                 && this.validCaveMaterial(posx, posy + 5, posz, world)
                                 && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 6, posz, world)
                                 && !isAboveFreeSpace(posx, posy + 5, posz, world)) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 5, posz);
                              }
                           }
                        }
                     }
                  }

                  if (!aDetBlock && world.field_73011_w.field_76574_g != 0) {
                     aBlock = world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz);
                     if (aBlock != null
                        && aBlock.func_149688_o().func_76220_a()
                        && aBlock.func_149662_c()
                        && !aBlock.func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                        && !aBlock.func_149716_u()) {
                        aMeta = world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz);
                        aDetBlock = true;
                     }
                  } else {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SlimeBlock);
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.SlimeBlock);
                     if (world.field_73011_w.field_76574_g == 0) {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
                     } else {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 2, posz, aBlock, aMeta, 0);
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (aDetBlock || world.field_73011_w.field_76574_g == 0) {
         for (int i = -1; i <= rad + 1; i++) {
            for (int j = -1; j <= rad / 4 + 2; j++) {
               for (int ex = -1; ex <= rad + 1; ex++) {
                  int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
                  int posy = yy - j;
                  int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SlimeBlock
                     && !this.validGroundMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world)
                     && (
                        world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.SlimeBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.SlimeBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.SlimeBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) == MFQM.SlimeBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) == MFQM.SlimeBlock
                     )) {
                     if (world.field_73011_w.field_76574_g == 0) {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
                     } else {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, aBlock, aMeta, 0);
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateCaveWebbing(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) {
                  if (j == 0 && this.validCaveMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world) && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                     if (this.validCaveMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world) && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                        if (random.nextInt(1) == 0
                           && this.validCaveMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)
                           && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 4, posz, world)) {
                           world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                           if (random.nextInt(2) == 0
                              && this.validCaveMaterial(posx, posy + 4, posz, world)
                              && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 5, posz, world)) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 4, posz);
                              if (random.nextInt(3) == 0
                                 && this.validCaveMaterial(posx, posy + 5, posz, world)
                                 && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 6, posz, world)) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 5, posz);
                              }
                           }
                        }
                     }
                  }

                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.DenseWebBlock);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.DenseWebBlock);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.DenseWebBlock
                  && !this.validGroundMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world)
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.DenseWebBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.DenseWebBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.DenseWebBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) == MFQM.DenseWebBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) == MFQM.DenseWebBlock
                  )) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateLiquidMire(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, Material BlockMaterialToReplace, int isWL) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.MireBlock
                        || BlockMaterialToReplace == Material.field_151568_F
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == BlockMaterialToReplace
                  )) {
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 6, posz) != MFQM.StableLiquidMireBlock) {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.StableLiquidMireBlock);
                     if (BlockMaterialToReplace == Material.field_151568_F
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz).func_149688_o() != BlockMaterialToReplace) {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.MireBlock);
                     }
                  }

                  if (isWL > 0 && random.nextInt(75 / isWL) == 0 && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == Blocks.field_150350_a) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 1, posz, MFQM.CustomLilyPadBlock, 0, 2);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      Block sblock = this.soilBlock(xx, zz, world);
      Block fblock = this.fillerBlock(xx, zz, world);

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.MireBlock
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.StableLiquidMireBlock
                  && (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.StableLiquidMireBlock || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.MireBlock)) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, fblock);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void spawnMorass(World world, int x, int y, int z, int ty) {
      if (ty == 1) {
         if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(5) == 0) {
            world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, world.field_73012_v.nextInt(2) + 7, 3);
         } else if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(3) == 0) {
            world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, world.field_73012_v.nextInt(4) + 3, 3);
         } else if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(2) == 0) {
            world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, 2, 3);
         } else if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(3) == 0) {
            world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, 0, 3);
         } else {
            world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, 1, 3);
         }
      } else if (ty == 2) {
         if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(20) == 0) {
            world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, world.field_73012_v.nextInt(2) + 7, 3);
         } else if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(10) == 0) {
            world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, world.field_73012_v.nextInt(4) + 3, 3);
         } else if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(5) == 0) {
            world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, 2, 3);
         } else if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(10) == 0) {
            world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, 0, 3);
         } else {
            world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, 1, 3);
         }
      } else if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(35) == 0) {
         world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, world.field_73012_v.nextInt(2) + 7, 3);
      } else if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(15) == 0) {
         world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, world.field_73012_v.nextInt(4) + 3, 3);
      } else if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(7) == 0) {
         world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, 2, 3);
      } else if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(15) == 0) {
         world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, 0, 3);
      } else {
         world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, MFQM.MorassBlock, 1, 3);
      }
   }

   private void generateMorass(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, float ty, int lush) {
      Block sblock = this.soilBlock(xx, zz, world);
      Block fblock = this.fillerBlock(xx, zz, world);
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      int dif = 0;
      int dif2 = 1;
      if (ty < 0.25) {
         if (random.nextInt(5) == 0) {
            dif = 1;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(10) == 0) {
            dif = 2;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(40) == 0) {
            dif2 = 2;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(80) == 0) {
            dif2 = 3;
         }
      }

      if (ty >= 0.25 && ty < 0.75) {
         if (random.nextInt(20) == 0) {
            dif = 1;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(40) == 0) {
            dif = 2;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(160) == 0) {
            dif2 = 2;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(320) == 0) {
            dif2 = 3;
         }
      }

      if (ty >= 0.75) {
         if (random.nextInt(40) == 0) {
            dif = 1;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(80) == 0) {
            dif = 2;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(320) == 0) {
            dif2 = 2;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(640) == 0) {
            dif2 = 3;
         }
      }

      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) {
                  boolean tickGen = false;
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.WetPeatBlock && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.StableLiquidMireBlock) {
                     if (j == 0) {
                        if ((
                              lush == 1
                                 || !this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world)
                                 || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.MorassBlock
                                 || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.MudBlock
                           )
                           && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                           if (this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                              if (isAboveFreeSpace(posx, posy + 2, posz, world) && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                                 this.spawnMorass(world, posx, posy, posz, Math.max(dif, dif2 - 1));
                                 world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.MireBlock, 10, 2);
                                 world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, fblock);
                                 tickGen = true;
                                 if (this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world) && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                                    world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                                 }
                              }
                           } else {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                              this.spawnMorass(world, posx, posy, posz, Math.max(dif, dif2 - 1));
                              world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.MireBlock, 10, 2);
                              world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, fblock);
                              tickGen = true;
                           }

                           if (random.nextInt(100 * dif2) == 0) {
                              ((BlockMorass)MFQM.MorassBlock).PlantingThis(world, random, posx, posy, posz);
                           } else {
                              if (random.nextInt(4 / dif2 + 1) == 0 && !this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                                 world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 1, posz, MFQM.MoorGrassBlock, 3, 2);
                              }

                              if (random.nextInt(30 * dif2) == 0 && !this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                                 world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 1, posz, MFQM.MoorGrassBlock, 2, 2);
                              }

                              if (random.nextInt(40 / (int)Math.floor(Math.pow(dif2, 2.0))) == 0 && !this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                                 if (random.nextInt(10) == 0) {
                                    world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 1, posz, MFQM.MoorGrassBlock, 4, 2);
                                 } else {
                                    world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 1, posz, MFQM.MoorGrassBlock, 5, 2);
                                 }
                              }
                           }
                        }
                     } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.MorassBlock || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.MireBlock) {
                        if (j == 1) {
                           world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MireBlock, 10, 0);
                        } else {
                           if (random.nextInt(8 / (dif + 1)) == 0) {
                              world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.WetPeatBlock);
                           } else {
                              world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MireBlock, 10, 0);
                              world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.WetPeatBlock);
                              world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, MFQM.PeatBlock);
                           }

                           tickGen = true;
                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.WetPeatBlock && random.nextInt(8 / (dif + 1)) == 0) {
                              world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.PeatBlock);
                           }

                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.PeatBlock) {
                              world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.PeatBlock);
                           }
                        }
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (lush == 1) {
         for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
               for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
                  int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
                  int posy = yy - j;
                  int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
                  if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()
                     && (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d() || world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz) != 0)
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.PeatBlock
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.WetPeatBlock
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.MireBlock
                     && (
                        world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.MireBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) != MFQM.WetPeatBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.MireBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) != MFQM.WetPeatBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) == MFQM.MireBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) != MFQM.WetPeatBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) == MFQM.MireBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) != MFQM.WetPeatBlock
                     )) {
                     if (this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, fblock);
                     } else {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, sblock);
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateWater(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, int wty) {
      boolean peat = false;
      Block sblock = this.soilBlock(xx, zz, world);
      Block fblock = this.fillerBlock(xx, zz, world);
      if (random.nextInt(10) == 0) {
         peat = true;
      }

      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) {
                  if (j == 0) {
                     if (this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                        if (isAboveFreeSpace(posx, posy + 3, posz, world) && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                           world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                           if (wty == 0) {
                              world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150355_j);
                           } else if (peat) {
                              if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                              }

                              world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.WetPeatBlock, 0, 2);
                              world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, fblock);
                           } else {
                              world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.StableLiquidMireBlock);
                              world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.StableLiquidMireBlock);
                              world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, MFQM.MireBlock);
                           }

                           if (this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world) && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                              if (this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world) && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 4, posz, world)) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                              }
                           }
                        }
                     } else {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                        if (wty == 0) {
                           world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150355_j);
                        } else if (peat) {
                           if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                           }

                           world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.WetPeatBlock, 0, 2);
                           world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, fblock);
                        } else {
                           world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.StableLiquidMireBlock);
                           world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.MireBlock);
                        }
                     }

                     if (random.nextInt(50) == 0 && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == Blocks.field_150350_a) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 1, posz, MFQM.CustomLilyPadBlock, 0, 2);
                     }
                  } else if (wty == 0) {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150355_j);
                  } else if (peat) {
                     if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                     }

                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.WetPeatBlock, 0, 2);
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, fblock);
                  } else {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.StableLiquidMireBlock);
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.MireBlock);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()
                  && (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d() || world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz) != 0)
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                  )) {
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == Blocks.field_150350_a) {
                     this.spawnMorass(world, posx, posy, posz, 0);
                  } else {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, fblock, 0, 0);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateTar(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.TarBlock);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.TarBlock);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.TarBlock
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.TarBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.TarBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.TarBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) == MFQM.TarBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) == MFQM.TarBlock
                  )) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateLarvae(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) {
                  boolean canGenW = true;
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  }

                  if (canGenW && this.validGroundMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.LarvaeBlock);
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.LarvaeBlock);
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, MFQM.LarvaeBlock);
                     if (this.validGroundMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world) && !this.isWoodNear(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                     }

                     if (this.validGroundMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world) && !this.isWoodNear(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                        boolean canGenW2 = true;
                        if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1 + 2, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW2 = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW2 = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW2 = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1 + 2, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW2 = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz - 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW2 = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz + 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW2 = false;
                        }

                        if (canGenW2) {
                           world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                        }
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      Block sblock = this.soilBlock(xx, zz, world);
      Block fblock = this.fillerBlock(xx, zz, world);

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 3; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.LarvaeBlock
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.LarvaeBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.LarvaeBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.LarvaeBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) == MFQM.LarvaeBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) == MFQM.LarvaeBlock
                  )) {
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, fblock);
                  } else {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, sblock);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateDenseWeb(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) {
                  boolean canGenW = true;
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  }

                  if (canGenW && this.validGroundMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world)) {
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != Blocks.field_150474_ac) {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.DenseWebBlock);
                     }

                     if (j == 0 && MFQM.GenWebU && random.nextInt(65) == 0) {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy + 2, posz, MFQM.DenseWebBlock);
                        world.func_147465_d(posx + 1, posy + 1, posz, MFQM.DenseWebBlock, 1, 2);
                        world.func_147465_d(posx - 1, posy + 1, posz, MFQM.DenseWebBlock, 1, 2);
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 1, posz, Blocks.field_150474_ac, 0, 2);
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 1, posz + 1, MFQM.DenseWebBlock, 1, 2);
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 1, posz - 1, MFQM.DenseWebBlock, 1, 2);
                        TileEntityMobSpawner var18 = (TileEntityMobSpawner)world.func_147438_o(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                        if (var18 != null) {
                           NBTTagCompound nbt = new NBTTagCompound();
                           var18.func_145841_b(nbt);
                           if (random.nextInt(5) == 0) {
                              nbt.func_74778_a("EntityId", "CaveSpider");
                              nbt.func_74777_a("MaxNearbyEntities", (short)16);
                           } else {
                              nbt.func_74778_a("EntityId", "Spider");
                              nbt.func_74777_a("MaxNearbyEntities", (short)10);
                           }

                           nbt.func_74777_a("RequiredPlayerRange", (short)96);
                           nbt.func_74777_a("SpawnRange", (short)32);
                           nbt.func_74777_a("MinSpawnDelay", (short)400);
                           nbt.func_74777_a("MaxSpawnDelay", (short)1600);
                           var18.func_145839_a(nbt);
                        } else {
                           System.err.println("(MFQM)Failed to fetch mob spawner entity");
                        }
                     }

                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.DenseWebBlock);
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, MFQM.DenseWebBlock);
                     if (this.validGroundMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world) && !this.isWoodNear(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                     }

                     if (this.validGroundMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world) && !this.isWoodNear(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                        boolean canGenW2 = true;
                        if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1 + 2, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW2 = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW2 = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW2 = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1 + 2, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW2 = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz - 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW2 = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz + 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW2 = false;
                        }

                        if (canGenW2) {
                           world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                        }
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      Block sblock = this.soilBlock(xx, zz, world);
      Block fblock = this.fillerBlock(xx, zz, world);

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 3; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != Blocks.field_150474_ac
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.DenseWebBlock
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.DenseWebBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.DenseWebBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.DenseWebBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) == MFQM.DenseWebBlock
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) == MFQM.DenseWebBlock
                  )) {
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, fblock);
                  } else {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, sblock);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateBog(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, Material BlockMaterialToReplace) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      Block sblock = this.soilBlock(xx, zz, world);
      Block fblock = this.fillerBlock(xx, zz, world);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= 5; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && (BlockMaterialToReplace == Material.field_151568_F || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == BlockMaterialToReplace)) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.BogBlock);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.BogBlock);
                  if (random.nextInt(75) == 0 && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == Blocks.field_150350_a) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 1, posz, MFQM.CustomLilyPadBlock, 0, 2);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= 7; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.BogBlock && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.BogBlock) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, fblock);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateSnow(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;
      int rady = rad;
      if (rad > 5 && random.nextInt(rad) != 0) {
         rady = 0;
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rady; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != Blocks.field_150350_a) {
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == Blocks.field_150431_aC) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SoftSnowBlock);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == Blocks.field_150431_aC) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.SoftSnowBlock);
                     if (rady == 0) {
                        rady = 1;
                     }
                  } else {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SoftSnowBlock);
                     if (rady > 1) {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.SoftSnowBlock);
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateGravel(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, boolean replace) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int j = 0; j <= rad; j++) {
         for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy + j - rad / 2;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) && replace) {
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateEmptyColumn(int xx, int yy, int zz, int len, World world, Random random) {
      for (int i = 0; i <= len; i++) {
         int posy = yy + i;
         world.func_147468_f(xx, posy, zz);
         if (i == len && this.validFallingMaterial(xx, posy + 1, zz, world)) {
            world.func_147449_b(xx, posy + 1, zz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateGravelColumn(int xx, int yy, int zz, int len, World world, Random random) {
      for (int i = 0; i <= len; i++) {
         int posy = yy - i;
         world.func_147468_f(xx, posy, zz);
      }
   }

   private void generateGravelPit(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, int depth) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;
      double minRad = rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0);
      int rad2 = rad * 2;
      double minRad2 = rad2 / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0);
      double midRad2 = minRad2 / 1.5;

      for (int j = 0; j <= rad2 / 4; j++) {
         for (int i = 0; i <= rad2; i++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad2; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad2 / 2;
               int posy = yy - j + rad2 / 8;
               int posz = zz + e - rad2 / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < minRad2) {
                  if (dis < midRad2) {
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SoftGravelBlock
                        && (this.validCaveMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world) || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d())) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SoftGravelBlock, 0, 2);
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, Blocks.field_150351_n);
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, Blocks.field_150351_n);
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 3, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 4, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 5, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
                     }
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SoftGravelBlock
                     && (this.validCaveMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world) || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d())) {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150351_n);
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 3, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
            int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
            int posy = yy;
            int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
            double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
            if (dis < minRad) {
               int len = (int)Math.floor((1.0 - dis / minRad) * depth);
               int plen = (int)Math.floor((1.0 - Math.max(dis - 1.0, 0.0) / minRad) * depth) + (int)Math.floor(Math.max(depth / 8.0, 2.0));
               int len2 = Math.max(plen - len, 0) + 5;
               len += random.nextInt((int)Math.floor(Math.max(depth / 8.0, 2.0)));
               this.generateGravelColumn(posx, yy, posz, len, world, random);
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, yy - len - 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                  for (int j = 0; j <= len2; j++) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - len - j, posz, MFQM.SoftGravelBlock, 0, 2);
                  }

                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - len - len2 - 1, posz, Blocks.field_150351_n);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - len - len2 - 2, posz, Blocks.field_150351_n);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - len - len2 - 3, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - len - len2 - 4, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - len - len2 - 5, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
               }

               this.generateEmptyColumn(posx, posy + 1, posz, len * 2, world, random);
            }
         }
      }

      for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
         for (int i = 0; i <= rad + 8; i++) {
            for (int exx = 0; exx <= rad + 8; exx++) {
               int siltDepth = yy - (int)Math.floor(depth * 0.667);
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2 - 4;
               int posy = siltDepth - j;
               int posz = zz + exx - rad / 2 - 4;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(siltDepth - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < minRad && posy <= siltDepth) {
                  if (posy == siltDepth && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SoftGravelBlock, 1, 2);
                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.SoftGravelBlock, 1, 2);
                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 2, posz, MFQM.SoftGravelBlock, 0, 2);
                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 3, posz, MFQM.SoftGravelBlock, 0, 2);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 4, posz, Blocks.field_150351_n);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 5, posz, Blocks.field_150351_n);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 6, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 7, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 8, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateSoftSand(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SandBlock);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.SandBlock);
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, Blocks.field_150322_A);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (rad > 4 && (rad < 10 || random.nextInt(2 + rad / 2) == 0)) {
         for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j <= rad + 1; j++) {
               for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
                  int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
                  int posy = yy - j;
                  int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
                  if (j < rad / 3 && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.SandBlock) {
                     boolean wall = false;
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) != Blocks.field_150350_a && world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) != MFQM.SandBlock) {
                        wall = true;
                     } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) != Blocks.field_150350_a
                        && world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) != MFQM.SandBlock) {
                        wall = true;
                     } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) != Blocks.field_150350_a
                        && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) != MFQM.SandBlock) {
                        wall = true;
                     } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) != Blocks.field_150350_a
                        && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) != MFQM.SandBlock) {
                        wall = true;
                     } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz) != Blocks.field_150350_a
                        && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz) != MFQM.SandBlock) {
                        wall = true;
                     }

                     if (!wall) {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateMoorGrass(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, Block BlockMaterialToReplace) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= 3; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == Blocks.field_150350_a
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz) == BlockMaterialToReplace
                  && random.nextInt(5) == 0) {
                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MoorGrassBlock, 1, 0);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateCaveWeb(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && random.nextInt(50) == 0
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == Blocks.field_150350_a
                  && (
                     this.validWebMaterial(posx - 1, posy, posz, world)
                        || this.validWebMaterial(posx + 1, posy, posz, world)
                        || this.validWebMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)
                        || this.validWebMaterial(posx, posy - 1, posz, world)
                        || this.validWebMaterial(posx, posy, posz - 1, world)
                        || this.validWebMaterial(posx, posy, posz + 1, world)
                  )) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150321_G);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateClay(int water, int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, int over) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      boolean isSinking = false;
      if (water == 1 && random.nextInt(2) == 0 && MFQM.GenSClay) {
         isSinking = true;
      }

      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;
      if (water == 1) {
         raz = 1.0;
         rax = 1.0;
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               int clayfens = (int)Math.floor(j / 3);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d
                  && !this.isWoodNear(posx, posy, posz, world)) {
                  if (over == 0) {
                     if (water == 0
                        && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()
                        && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SClayBlock) {
                        boolean canGenW = true;
                        if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW = false;
                        } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                           canGenW = false;
                        }

                        if (canGenW) {
                           if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                           }

                           if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                           }

                           if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                           }

                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()
                              && (random.nextInt(clayfens + 1) != 0 || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) != MFQM.HClayBlock)
                              && clayfens != 0
                              && random.nextInt(clayfens + 1) != 0) {
                              world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150435_aG);
                           } else if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a() && random.nextInt(2) == 0) {
                              world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.HClayBlock, random.nextInt(5), 0);
                           } else {
                              world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.HClayBlock);
                           }
                        }
                     }
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()
                        && (random.nextInt(clayfens + 1) != 0 || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) != MFQM.HClayBlock)
                        && clayfens != 0
                        && random.nextInt(clayfens + 1) != 0) {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150435_aG);
                     } else if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a() && random.nextInt(2) == 0) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.HClayBlock, random.nextInt(5), 0);
                     } else {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.HClayBlock);
                     }
                  }

                  if (water == 1) {
                     boolean canGenWx = true;
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
                        canGenWx = false;
                     }

                     if (canGenWx) {
                        if ((!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d() || !isSinking)
                           && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) != MFQM.SClayBlock) {
                           if (!isSinking && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
                              if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                              }

                              if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                              }

                              if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                              }

                              if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()
                                 && (random.nextInt(clayfens + 1) != 0 || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) != MFQM.HClayBlock)
                                 && clayfens != 0
                                 && random.nextInt(clayfens + 1) != 0) {
                                 world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150435_aG);
                              } else if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a() && random.nextInt(5) == 0) {
                                 world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.HClayBlock, random.nextInt(5), 0);
                              } else {
                                 world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.HClayBlock);
                              }
                           }
                        } else {
                           if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                           }

                           if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                           }

                           if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                           }

                           if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                              if (random.nextInt(10) == 0) {
                                 world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SClayBlock, random.nextInt(5), 0);
                              } else {
                                 world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SClayBlock, 0, 0);
                              }

                              world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.SClayBlock, 7, 0);
                           } else {
                              world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SClayBlock, 7, 0);
                           }
                        }
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.HClayBlock || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.SClayBlock) {
                  boolean canGenWxx = true;
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenWxx = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenWxx = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenWxx = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenWxx = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenWxx = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenWxx = false;
                  }

                  if (!canGenWxx) {
                     int inThisBlocks = 0;
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151586_h) {
                        inThisBlocks++;
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151586_h) {
                        inThisBlocks++;
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151586_h) {
                        inThisBlocks++;
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151586_h) {
                        inThisBlocks++;
                     }

                     if (inThisBlocks > 1) {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150435_aG);
                     } else {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150369_x);
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int exx = 0; exx <= rad; exx++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + exx - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == Blocks.field_150369_x) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150358_i);
               }
            }
