      }
   }

   private void generateOnlyClay(boolean sink, int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = 1.0;
      double raz = 1.0;
      boolean isSinking = sink;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               int clayfens = (int)Math.floor(j / 3);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SClayBlock
                  && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                  && !this.isWoodNear(posx, posy, posz, world)) {
                  boolean canGenW = true;
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenW = false;
                  }

                  if (isSinking) {
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                        isSinking = false;
                     } else if (!world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                        isSinking = false;
                     } else if (!world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                        isSinking = false;
                     } else if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                        isSinking = false;
                     } else if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                        isSinking = false;
                     } else if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                        isSinking = false;
                     }
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.SClayBlock) {
                     isSinking = true;
                  }

                  if (canGenW) {
                     if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                     }

                     if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                     }

                     if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                     }

                     if (isSinking) {
                        if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                           if (random.nextInt(10) == 0) {
                              world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SClayBlock, random.nextInt(5), 0);
                           } else {
                              world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SClayBlock, 0, 0);
                           }

                           world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.SClayBlock, 7, 0);
                        } else {
                           world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.SClayBlock, 7, 0);
                        }
                     } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()
                        && (random.nextInt(1 + clayfens) != 0 || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) != MFQM.HClayBlock)
                        && clayfens != 0
                        && random.nextInt(clayfens + 1) != 0) {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150435_aG);
                     } else if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a() && random.nextInt(5) == 0) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.HClayBlock, random.nextInt(5), 0);
                     } else {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.HClayBlock);
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.HClayBlock || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.SClayBlock) {
                  boolean canGenWx = true;
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenWx = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenWx = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenWx = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenWx = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenWx = false;
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                     canGenWx = false;
                  }

                  if (!canGenWx) {
                     int inThisBlocks = 0;
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151586_h) {
                        inThisBlocks++;
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151586_h) {
                        inThisBlocks++;
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151586_h) {
                        inThisBlocks++;
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151586_h) {
                        inThisBlocks++;
                     }

                     if (inThisBlocks > 1) {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150435_aG);
                     } else {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150369_x);
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int exx = 0; exx <= rad; exx++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + exx - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == Blocks.field_150369_x) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, Blocks.field_150358_i);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void ClearArea(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy + j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) {
                  world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private int ClearArea2(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      int removedMouth = 0;
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
         for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy + j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               double disa = rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0);
               if (dis < disa) {
                  if (dis > disa - 1.5) {
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 0, 2);
                     } else {
                        if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.MeatBlock && world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz) == 10) {
                           world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 1, posz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 10, 2);
                           this.ClearArea3(posx, posy + 1, posz, 5 + random.nextInt(5), world, random);
                        }

                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
                     }
                  } else {
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.MeatBlock && world.func_72805_g(posx, posy, posz) == 10) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy + 1, posz, MFQM.MeatBlock, 10, 2);
                        this.ClearArea3(posx, posy + 1, posz, 5 + random.nextInt(5), world, random);
                     }

                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      return removedMouth;
   }

   private void spawnGreenStone(int x, int y, int z, World world, Random random, int cof) {
      switch (cof) {
         case 0:
            if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(15) == 0) {
               world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, Blocks.field_150348_b, 0, 0);
               return;
            } else {
               if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(15) == 0) {
                  world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, Blocks.field_150347_e, 0, 0);
                  return;
               }

               world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, Blocks.field_150341_Y, 0, 0);
               return;
            }
         case 1:
            if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(1) == 0) {
               world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, Blocks.field_150341_Y, 0, 0);
               return;
            } else {
               if (world.field_73012_v.nextInt(2) == 0) {
                  world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, Blocks.field_150347_e, 0, 0);
                  return;
               }

               world.func_147465_d(x, y, z, Blocks.field_150348_b, 0, 0);
               return;
            }
      }
   }

   private void spawnSoftQuicksand(int x, int y, int z, World world, Random random, int cof) {

   }

   private void generateSoftQuicksandForest(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, Material BlockMaterialToReplace, Block MireBlockId) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                  && (BlockMaterialToReplace == Material.field_151568_F || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == BlockMaterialToReplace)) {
                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k() || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MireBlockId) {
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MireBlockId);
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 1, posz, MireBlockId);
                     world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateSoftQuicksand(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, int ssg) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) {
                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (j == 0) {
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                        if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d) {
                           world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz), world.func_72805_g(posx, posy + 1, posz), 2);
                        } else if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 3, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                           world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
                           world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                           world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                        }
                     }

                     int re = world.field_73012_v.nextInt(4);
                     switch (re) {
                        case 0:
                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                              if (!world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy + 3, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx - 1, posy, posz);
                              }

                              this.spawnGreenStone(posx - 1, posy - 1, posz, world, random, 0);
                           }
                           break;
                        case 1:
                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                              if (!world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy + 3, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx + 1, posy, posz);
                              }

                              this.spawnGreenStone(posx + 1, posy - 1, posz, world, random, 0);
                           }
                           break;
                        case 2:
                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                              if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 3, posz - 1).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz - 1);
                              }

                              this.spawnGreenStone(posx, posy - 1, posz - 1, world, random, 0);
                           }
                           break;
                        case 3:
                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                              if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 3, posz + 1).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz + 1);
                              }

                              this.spawnGreenStone(posx, posy - 1, posz + 1, world, random, 0);
                           }
                     }
                  } else if (ssg == 0) {
                     this.spawnSoftQuicksand(posx, posy, posz, world, random, 1);
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock) {
                     this.spawnGreenStone(posx, posy, posz, world, random, 0);
                  }

                  if (ssg == 0) {
                     this.spawnSoftQuicksand(posx, posy - 1, posz, world, random, 1);
                  } else if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz) != MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock) {
                     this.spawnGreenStone(posx, posy - 1, posz, world, random, 0);
                  }

                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 2, posz, Blocks.field_150348_b);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 3; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock) {
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                     || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                     || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                     || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) == MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                     || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) == MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock) {
                     this.spawnGreenStone(posx, posy, posz, world, random, 0);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz) != MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                     && (
                        world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy - 1, posz) == MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy - 1, posz) == MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz - 1) == MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz + 1) == MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                     )) {
                     this.spawnGreenStone(posx, posy, posz, world, random, 0);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateFlatTar(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, int ssg, Block filler, int metaFiller) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) {
                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (j == 0) {
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                        if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d) {
                           world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz), world.func_72805_g(posx, posy + 1, posz), 2);
                        } else if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 3, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                           if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149716_u()) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
                           }

                           if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149716_u()) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
                           }

                           if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz).func_149716_u()) {
                              world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                           }
                        }
                     }

                     if (MFQM.EUD
                        && world.field_73011_w.field_76574_g == MFQM.EUDDim
                        && this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)
                        && !this.validSolidMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                     }

                     int re = world.field_73012_v.nextInt(4);
                     switch (re) {
                        case 0:
                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()
                              && !world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz).func_149716_u()) {
                              if (!world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy + 3, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx - 1, posy, posz);
                              }

                              world.func_147465_d(posx - 1, posy - 1, posz, filler, metaFiller, 2);
                           }
                           break;
                        case 1:
                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()
                              && !world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz).func_149716_u()) {
                              if (!world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy + 3, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx + 1, posy, posz);
                              }

                              world.func_147465_d(posx + 1, posy - 1, posz, filler, metaFiller, 2);
                           }
                           break;
                        case 2:
                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()
                              && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1).func_149716_u()) {
                              if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 3, posz - 1).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz - 1);
                              }

                              world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 1, posz - 1, filler, metaFiller, 2);
                           }
                           break;
                        case 3:
                           if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()
                              && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1).func_149716_u()) {
                              if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 3, posz + 1).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                                 world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz + 1);
                              }

                              world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 1, posz + 1, filler, metaFiller, 2);
                           }
                     }
                  } else if (ssg == 0) {
                     if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149716_u()) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.TarBlock, 0, 2);
                     }
                  } else if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149716_u() && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.TarBlock) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, filler, metaFiller, 2);
                  }

                  if (ssg == 0) {
                     if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz).func_149716_u()) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.TarBlock, 0, 2);
                     }
                  } else if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149716_u() && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz) != MFQM.TarBlock) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, filler, metaFiller, 2);
                  }

                  if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 2, posz).func_149716_u()) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 2, posz, filler, metaFiller, 2);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 3; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d
                  && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149716_u()
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.TarBlock) {
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.TarBlock
                     || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) == MFQM.TarBlock
                     || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) == MFQM.TarBlock
                     || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) == MFQM.TarBlock
                     || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) == MFQM.TarBlock) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, filler, metaFiller, 2);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz) != MFQM.TarBlock
                     && (
                        world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy - 1, posz) == MFQM.TarBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy - 1, posz) == MFQM.TarBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz - 1) == MFQM.TarBlock
                           || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz + 1) == MFQM.TarBlock
                     )) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, filler, metaFiller, 2);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateCliffsTar(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, Block filler, int metaFiller) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                  )
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.TarBlock
                  && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149716_u()
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, filler, metaFiller, 2);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateRockTar(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, Block filler, int metaFiller) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int j = rad / 4; j >= 0; j--) {
         for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy + j - rad / 8;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow((yy - posy) * 4, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) != MFQM.TarBlock
                  && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149716_u()) {
                  world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, filler, metaFiller, 2);
                  world.func_147468_f(posx, posy - 1, posz);
                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 1, posz, filler, metaFiller, 2);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateCliffs(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                  )
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                  this.spawnGreenStone(posx, posy, posz, world, random, 0);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateRock(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 4; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy + j - rad / 8;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow((yy - posy) * 4, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) {
                  this.spawnGreenStone(posx, posy, posz, world, random, 0);
                  this.spawnGreenStone(posx, posy - 1, posz, world, random, 0);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateMoss(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      Block sblock = this.soilBlock(xx, zz, world);
      Block fblock = this.fillerBlock(xx, zz, world);
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      boolean aTBL = world.field_73011_w.field_76574_g == MFQM.TBLDim;
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= 0; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && (
                     world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == Blocks.field_150350_a
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151585_k
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151582_l
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76222_j()
                        || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                  )
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
                  world.func_147449_b(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.MossBlock);
                  if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 2, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 2, posz, world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz), 0, 0);
                     if (aTBL) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 3, posz, MFQM.MudBlock, 3, 0);
                     } else {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 3, posz, fblock);
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy - 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                        && !world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy - 2, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx + 1, posy - 2, posz, world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz), 0, 0);
                        if (aTBL) {
                           world.func_147465_d(posx + 1, posy - 3, posz, MFQM.MudBlock, 3, 0);
                        } else {
                           world.func_147449_b(posx + 1, posy - 3, posz, fblock);
                        }
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy - 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                        && !world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy - 2, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx - 1, posy - 2, posz, world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz), 0, 0);
                        if (aTBL) {
                           world.func_147465_d(posx - 1, posy - 3, posz, MFQM.MudBlock, 3, 0);
                        } else {
                           world.func_147449_b(posx - 1, posy - 3, posz, fblock);
                        }
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz - 1).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                        && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 2, posz - 1).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 2, posz - 1, world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz), 0, 0);
                        if (aTBL) {
                           world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 3, posz - 1, MFQM.MudBlock, 3, 0);
                        } else {
                           world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 3, posz - 1, fblock);
                        }
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz + 1).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                        && !world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 2, posz + 1).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
                        world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 2, posz + 1, world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz), 0, 0);
                        if (aTBL) {
                           world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 3, posz + 1, MFQM.MudBlock, 3, 0);
                        } else {
                           world.func_147449_b(posx, posy - 3, posz + 1, fblock);
                        }
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private boolean generateWaxTree(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      int waxx = 0;
      int waxy = 0;
      int waxz = 0;
      int[] sides = new int[4];
      int count = 0;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad && waxy == 0; i++) {
         for (int e = 0; e <= rad && waxy == 0; e++) {
            int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
            int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
            if (world.func_147439_a(posx, yy, posz) == Blocks.field_150364_r && world.func_72805_g(posx, yy, posz) == 3) {
               int tmpCheck = 0;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, yy, posz) == Blocks.field_150350_a
                  || world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, yy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76222_j()
                  || world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, yy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151582_l) {
                  tmpCheck++;
                  sides[0] = 1;
               }

               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, yy, posz + 1) == Blocks.field_150350_a
                  || world.func_147439_a(posx, yy, posz + 1).func_149688_o().func_76222_j()
                  || world.func_147439_a(posx, yy, posz + 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151582_l) {
                  tmpCheck++;
                  sides[1] = 2;
               }

               if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, yy, posz) == Blocks.field_150350_a
                  || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, yy, posz).func_149688_o().func_76222_j()
                  || world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, yy, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151582_l) {
                  tmpCheck++;
                  sides[2] = 3;
               }

               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, yy, posz - 1) == Blocks.field_150350_a
                  || world.func_147439_a(posx, yy, posz - 1).func_149688_o().func_76222_j()
                  || world.func_147439_a(posx, yy, posz - 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151582_l) {
                  tmpCheck++;
                  sides[3] = 4;
               }

               if (tmpCheck > 0) {
                  waxx = posx;
                  waxy = yy;
                  waxz = posz;
                  count = tmpCheck;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (waxy == 0) {
         return false;
      } else {
         for (int i = 0; i < 25; i++) {
            if (world.func_147439_a(waxx, waxy + i, waxz) == MFQM.WaxWoodBlock) {
               return false;
            }

            if (world.func_147439_a(waxx, waxy - i, waxz) == MFQM.WaxWoodBlock) {
               return false;
            }

            if (world.func_147439_a(waxx + 1, waxy, waxz) == MFQM.WaxWoodBlock) {
               return false;
            }

            if (world.func_147439_a(waxx - 1, waxy, waxz) == MFQM.WaxWoodBlock) {
               return false;
            }

            if (world.func_147439_a(waxx, waxy, waxz + 1) == MFQM.WaxWoodBlock) {
               return false;
            }

            if (world.func_147439_a(waxx, waxy, waxz - 1) == MFQM.WaxWoodBlock) {
               return false;
            }
         }

         int[] sidesTrue = new int[count];
         int j = 0;

         for (int i = 0; i < sides.length; i++) {
            if (sides[i] != 0) {
               sidesTrue[j] = sides[i];
               j++;
            }
         }

         int side = random.nextInt(count);
         int meta = -1;
         int waxxx = waxx;
         int waxyy = waxy;
         int waxzz = waxz;
         byte var31;
         switch (sidesTrue[side]) {
            case 1:
               if (world.func_147439_a(waxx + 1, waxy, waxz) != Blocks.field_150364_r) {
                  return false;
               }

               var31 = 1;
               waxxx = waxx - 1;
               break;
            case 2:
               if (world.func_147439_a(waxx, waxy, waxz - 1) != Blocks.field_150364_r) {
                  return false;
               }

               var31 = 0;
               waxzz = waxz + 1;
               break;
            case 3:
               if (world.func_147439_a(waxx - 1, waxy, waxz) != Blocks.field_150364_r) {
                  return false;
               }

               var31 = 3;
               waxxx = waxx + 1;
               break;
            case 4:
               if (world.func_147439_a(waxx, waxy, waxz + 1) != Blocks.field_150364_r) {
                  return false;
               }

               var31 = 2;
               waxzz = waxz - 1;
               break;
            default:
               return false;
         }

         world.func_147465_d(waxx, waxy, waxz, MFQM.WaxWoodBlock, var31, 0);
         world.func_147468_f(waxxx, waxy, waxzz);
         boolean genHill = false;

         while (waxyy > 60) {
            if (world.func_147439_a(waxx, --waxyy, waxz) != Blocks.field_150364_r) {
               genHill = true;
               break;
            }

            world.func_147465_d(waxx, waxyy, waxz, MFQM.WaxWoodBlock, var31 + 4, 0);
            if (!world.func_147439_a(waxxx, waxyy, waxzz).func_149688_o().func_76222_j()
               && world.func_147439_a(waxxx, waxyy, waxzz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151582_l
               && world.func_147439_a(waxxx, waxyy, waxzz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151584_j
               && world.func_147439_a(waxxx, waxyy, waxzz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151585_k) {
               genHill = true;
               break;
            }

            world.func_147468_f(waxxx, waxyy, waxzz);
         }

         if (genHill && waxyy < 70) {
            int rada = random.nextInt(4) + 15;
            int xpos = waxx + (waxxx - waxx) * (rada / 5);
            int zpos = waxz + (waxzz - waxz) * (rada / 5);
            this.generateHill(xpos, waxyy + 1, zpos, rada, world, random);
            int hc = random.nextInt(2) + 5;

            for (int ix = 0; ix < hc; ix++) {
               int rada2 = random.nextInt(4) + 4;
               this.generateHill(
                  xpos + random.nextInt(rada / 2 + 1) - rada / 4,
                  waxyy + random.nextInt(3) - 1,
                  zpos + random.nextInt(rada / 2 + 1) - rada / 4,
                  rada2,
                  world,
                  random
               );
            }

            xpos = waxx + (waxxx - waxx) * 2;
            zpos = waxz + (waxzz - waxz) * 2;
            world.func_147465_d(waxxx, waxyy, waxzz, MFQM.WaxBlock, 4, 0);
            hc = random.nextInt(2) + 1;

            for (int ix = 0; ix < hc; ix++) {
               int rada2 = random.nextInt(2) + 4;
               this.generateWax(xpos, waxyy, zpos, rada2, world, random);
            }
         }

         return true;
      }
   }

   private void generateWax(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
      double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)
                  && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) != MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock) {
                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 2, posz);
                  }

                  if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 3, posz);
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151575_d
                     && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 2, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 1, posz);
                  }

                  world.func_147465_d(posx, posy, posz, MFQM.WaxBlock, 4, 0);
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz) == Blocks.field_150350_a
                     || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76222_j()
                     || world.func_147439_a(posx, posy - 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151582_l) {
                     world.func_147465_d(posx, posy - 1, posz, MFQM.WaxBlock, 5, 0);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateHill(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
      double rad_cof = Math.max(rad / 10.0, 1.0);
      double rax = 1.0;
      double raz = 1.0;

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad * 2; j++) {
            for (int e = 0; e <= rad; e++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j + rad;
               int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
               double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0));
               if (dis < rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0) && j > rad - rad / 4) {
                  int posyp = posy - rad / 4;
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posyp, posz).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151575_d) {
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posyp + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76218_k()) {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posyp, posz, Blocks.field_150346_d);
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posyp - 1, posz, Blocks.field_150346_d);
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posyp - 2, posz, Blocks.field_150346_d);
                     } else if (!world.func_147439_a(posx, posyp + 1, posz).func_149688_o().func_76224_d()) {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posyp, posz, Blocks.field_150349_c);
                        if (random.nextInt(5) == 0) {
                           Blocks.field_150349_c.func_149853_b(world, random, posx, posy, posz);
                        }

                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posyp - 1, posz, Blocks.field_150346_d);
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posyp - 2, posz, Blocks.field_150346_d);
                     } else {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posyp, posz, Blocks.field_150346_d);
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posyp - 1, posz, Blocks.field_150346_d);
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posyp - 2, posz, Blocks.field_150346_d);
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      for (int i = 0; i <= rad; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j <= rad * 2 + 2; j++) {
            for (int ex = 0; ex <= rad; ex++) {
               int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
               int posy = yy - j + rad;
               int posz = zz + ex - rad / 2;
               int posyp = posy - rad / 4;
               if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posyp, posz) == Blocks.field_150349_c || world.func_147439_a(posx, posyp, posz) == Blocks.field_150346_d) {
                  int cnt = 0;
                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posyp, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                     cnt++;
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posyp, posz).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                     cnt++;
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posyp, posz - 1).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                     cnt++;
                  }

                  if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posyp, posz + 1).func_149688_o().func_76220_a()) {
                     cnt++;
                  }

                  if (cnt < 2) {
                     int cnt2 = 0;
                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posyp, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                        cnt2++;
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posyp, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                        cnt2++;
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posyp, posz - 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                        cnt2++;
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posyp, posz + 1).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                        cnt2++;
                     }

                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posyp + 1, posz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151586_h) {
                        cnt2++;
                     }

                     if (cnt2 > 0) {
                        world.func_147449_b(posx, posyp, posz, Blocks.field_150358_i);
                     } else {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posyp, posz);
                        if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posyp - 1, posz) == Blocks.field_150346_d) {
                           world.func_147449_b(posx, posyp - 1, posz, Blocks.field_150349_c);
                        }
                     }

                     if (this.validPlantMaterial(posx, posyp + 1, posz, world)) {
                        world.func_147468_f(posx, posyp + 1, posz);
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private int getSwampType(int ui) {
      if (this.T_BoP) {
         if (ui == MFQM.SwampBiomes[12] || ui == MFQM.SwampBiomes[11] || ui == MFQM.SwampBiomes[10] || ui == MFQM.SwampBiomes[3] || ui == MFQM.SwampBiomes[0]) {
            return 2;
         } else {
            return ui == MFQM.SwampBiomes[2] ? 1 : 0;
         }
      } else {
         return 2;
      }
   }

   private boolean isSwampIsNear(int xx, int yy, int zz, World world) {
      int xxx = xx - 32;
      int zzz = zz - 32;

      for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j < 5; j++) {
            xxx = xx - 32 + 16 * i;
            zzz = zz - 32 + 16 * j;
            if (xxx != xx || zzz != zz) {
               BiomeGenBase bb = world.func_72807_a(xxx, zzz);
               if (bb instanceof BiomeGenSwamp) {
                  return true;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private boolean isJungleIsNear(int xx, int yy, int zz, World world) {
      int xxx = xx - 16;
      int zzz = zz - 16;

      for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
         for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++) {
            xxx = xx - 16 + 16 * i;
            zzz = zz - 16 + 16 * j;
            if (xxx != xx || zzz != zz) {
               BiomeGenBase bb = world.func_72807_a(xxx, zzz);
               if (bb instanceof BiomeGenJungle) {
                  return true;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private int isTarBiomeIsNear(int xx, int yy, int zz, World world) {
      int xxx = xx - 32;
      int zzz = zz - 32;
      if (this.T_BoP) {
         for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < 5; j++) {
               xxx = xx - 32 + 16 * i;
               zzz = zz - 32 + 16 * j;
               BiomeGenBase bb = world.func_72807_a(xxx, zzz);
               if (this.getTarCount(bb.field_76756_M) > 0) {
                  return this.getTarCount(bb.field_76756_M);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      return 0;
   }

   private boolean isRiverIsNear(int xx, int yy, int zz, World world) {
      int xxx = xx - 32;
      int zzz = zz - 32;
      if (this.T_BoP) {
         for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < 5; j++) {

               zzz = zz - 32 + 16 * j;
               if (xxx != xx || zzz != zz) {
                  BiomeGenBase bb = world.func_72807_a(xxx, zzz);
                  if (bb instanceof BiomeGenRiver) {
                     return true;
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private Vec3 getXcor(World world, int x, int y, int z, int rad) {
      int xinc = 0;
      int xdec = 0;

      for (int i = 1; i < rad; i++) {
         if (xinc == 0 && !isAboveFreeSpace(x + i, y + 3, z, world)) {
            xinc = i - 1;
         }

         if (xdec == 0 && !isAboveFreeSpace(x - i, y + 3, z, world)) {
            xdec = i - 1;
         }

         if (xdec != 0 && xinc == 0) {
            return Vec3.func_72443_a((xinc + xdec) / 2 - xdec, (xinc + xdec) / 2, 0.0);
         }
      }

      return Vec3.func_72443_a(0.0, rad, 0.0);
   }

   private Vec3 getZcor(World world, int x, int y, int z, int rad) {
      int zinc = 0;
      int zdec = 0;

      for (int i = 1; i < rad; i++) {
         if (zinc == 0 && !isAboveFreeSpace(x, y + 3, z + i, world)) {
            zinc = i - 1;
         }

         if (zdec == 0 && !isAboveFreeSpace(x, y + 3, z - i, world)) {
            zdec = i - 1;
         }

         if (zdec != 0 && zinc == 0) {
            return Vec3.func_72443_a((zinc + zdec) / 2 - zdec, (zinc + zdec) / 2, 0.0);
         }
      }

      return Vec3.func_72443_a(0.0, rad, 0.0);
   }

   private int getPeatBogType(int ui) {
      if (this.T_BoP) {
         if (ui == MFQM.PeatBogBiomes[1] || ui == MFQM.PeatBogBiomes[4]) {
            return 1;
         }

         if (ui == MFQM.PeatBogBiomes[3]) {
            return 0;
         }

         if (ui == MFQM.PeatBogBiomes[5] || ui == MFQM.PeatBogBiomes[6]) {
            return 3;
         }
      }

      return 0;
   }

   private boolean getPeatBogGenBirch(int ui) {
      if (this.T_BoP) {
         for (int O = 0; O < 7; O++) {
            if (ui == MFQM.PeatBogBiomes[O]) {
               if (O < 3) {
                  return true;
               }

               return false;
            }
         }
      }

      return false;
   }

   private int getForestTypeBOP(int ui) {
      return !this.T_BoP
            || ui != MFQM.SQSBiomes[0]
               && ui != MFQM.SQSBiomes[1]
               && ui != MFQM.SQSBiomes[2]
               && ui != MFQM.SQSBiomes[3]
               && ui != MFQM.SQSBiomes[4]
               && ui != MFQM.SQSBiomes[5]
               && ui != MFQM.SQSBiomes[6]
               && ui != MFQM.SQSBiomes[7]
               && ui != MFQM.SQSBiomes[8]
               && ui != MFQM.SQSBiomes[9]
         ? 0
         : 1;
   }

   private void generateAOADimVoxPonds(World world, Random random, int chunkX, int chunkZ) {
      int dHeight = 0;
      int firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
      int firstBlockYCoord = 96;
      int firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
      BiomeGenBase biomegenbase = world.func_72807_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
      int biomeId = biomegenbase.field_76756_M;
      boolean blockGen = false;
      int depth = random.nextInt(4);

      for (int h = 0; h < 16; h++) {
         for (int v = 0; v < 16; v++) {
            firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + h;
            firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + v;

            for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
               firstBlockYCoord = 37 + dHeight - k;
               if (world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord) == MFQM.AOAToxicWaste) {
                  if (k != 0 && depth != 3) {
                     world.func_147465_d(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, MFQM.MudBlock, 0, 0);
                  } else {
                     world.func_147465_d(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, MFQM.MudBlock, depth, 0);
                  }
               }
            }

            if (this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, 17, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
               world.func_147465_d(firstBlockXCoord, 16, firstBlockZCoord, MFQM.MudBlock, 3, 0);
               world.func_147465_d(firstBlockXCoord, 15, firstBlockZCoord, MFQM.MudBlock, 3, 0);
               world.func_147465_d(firstBlockXCoord, 14, firstBlockZCoord, MFQM.MudBlock, 3, 0);
               world.func_147465_d(firstBlockXCoord, 13, firstBlockZCoord, Blocks.field_150357_h, 0, 0);
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenWastePit && !blockGen) {
         boolean dep = false;
         if (random.nextInt(10) == 0) {
            dep = true;
         }

         boolean canGen = false;
         int genCount = 30 + random.nextInt(30);
         int genPos = 10;
         int genRad = 7;
         int genRadP = 7;
         int rad = 0;
         if (random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 48 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 36 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validSolidMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 5) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen
                  && this.genIsFlatSlurry(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                  && this.genIsFlatSlurry(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                  if (dep) {
                     this.generateMireOD(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.SlurryBlock, 0);
                  } else {
                     this.generateMireO(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.SlurryBlock);
                  }

                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

               if (canGen && this.isGroundQS(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, (int)Math.round(rad * 1.5), world, MFQM.MorassBlock)) {
                  if (BGPX == 0) {
                     BGPX = firstBlockXCoord;
                     BGPZ = firstBlockZCoord;
                  }

                  float disToC = Math.max((float)Math.sqrt(Math.pow(firstBlockXCoord - BGPX, 2.0) + Math.pow(firstBlockZCoord - BGPZ, 2.0)), 1.0F);
                  if (random.nextInt(100) == 0) {
                     this.generateWater(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, Math.min(rad, 7), world, random, 1);
                  } else {
                     this.generateMorass(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, 18.0F / disToC, 0);
                  }

                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      var23 = 0;
      if (MFQM.GenLiqMire && !blockGen) {
         boolean canGenx = false;
         int genCount = 0;
         int genPos = 0;
         int genRad = 0;
         int genRadP = 0;
         int radx = 0;
         genCount = 20 + random.nextInt(10);
         int var62 = 10;
         int var66 = 10;
         int var58 = 2;
         if (random.nextInt(var58) == 0) {

               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + var23;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);

               for (canGenx = false; firstBlockYCoord > 66 + var23; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord) == Blocks.field_150355_j

   private void generateAOADimLandsLelyetia(World world, Random random, int chunkX, int chunkZ) {
      int dHeight = 0;
      boolean blockGen = false;
      if (MFQM.GenSSnow) {
         int depth = 0;
         if (random.nextInt(5) == 0) {
            depth = 1 + random.nextInt(4);
         }

         for (int h = 0; h < 16; h++) {
            for (int v = 0; v < 16; v++) {
               int firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + h;
               int firstBlockYCoord = 57;
               int firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + v;
               if (depth == 0) {
                  if (!this.validSolidMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     world.func_147465_d(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, MFQM.SoftSnowBlock, 1, 2);
                  }
               } else if (depth == 1) {
                  if (!this.validSolidMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     world.func_147465_d(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, MFQM.SoftSnowBlock, 1, 2);
                  }

                  if (!this.validSolidMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     world.func_147465_d(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, MFQM.SoftSnowBlock, 1, 2);
                  }

                  if (!this.validSolidMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord - 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     world.func_147465_d(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord - 1, firstBlockZCoord, MFQM.SoftSnowBlock, 1, 2);
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

               if (canGen) {
                  if (this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                     && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad - 4, world)) {
                     if (Flat) {
                        this.generateMireM(
                           firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151578_c, MFQM.SinkingWoolBlock, aMeta
                        );
                     } else {
                        this.generateMireM(
                           firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151578_c, Blocks.field_150325_L, aMeta
                        );
                     }

                     blockGen = true;
                  }
               } else if (firstBlockYCoord < 17 + dHeight) {
                  break;
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

                     world.func_147465_d(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, MFQM.TarBlock, 0, 2);

   }

   private void generateAOADimCreeponia(World world, Random random, int chunkX, int chunkZ) {

      int biomeId = biomegenbase.field_76756_M;
      boolean blockGen = false;

      if (MFQM.GenTar && !blockGen) {
         int trType = 0;

         int tarP2 = 0;
         int tarPU = 0;

            blockGen = true;
         }

      if (MFQM.GenSQSF && !blockGen && isJungle) {
         boolean canGen = false;
         int genCount = 0;
         int genPos = 0;
         int genRad = 0;
         int genRadP = 0;

         int var43 = 8;
         int var47 = 8;
         int var39 = 80;
         if (random.nextInt(var39) == 0) {

                  if (this.validGroundMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen
                  && this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                  && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                  this.generateSoftQuicksandForest(
                     firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                  );
                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenJQS && !blockGen && isJungle) {
         boolean canGen = false;
         int genCount = 10 + random.nextInt(10);
         int genPos = 30;
         int genRad = 8;
         int genRadP = 10;
         int rad = 0;
         if (random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
            boolean Flat = false;
            if (random.nextInt(10) == 0) {
               Flat = true;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);

                  if (world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord) == MFQM.TarBlock) {
                     canGen = false;

                  }

                  if (this.validGroundMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen
                  && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                  && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {

                        firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.JungleQuicksandBlock
                     );

                     this.generateMireOD(
                        firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.JungleQuicksandBlock, 0
                     );

                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateAOADimLBorean(World world, Random random, int chunkX, int chunkZ) {
      int dHeight = 0;
      int firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
      int firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
      int firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
      BiomeGenBase biomegenbase = world.func_72807_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
      int biomeId = biomegenbase.field_76756_M;
      boolean blockGen = false;
      boolean isRedForest = biomeId == MFQM.AOABiomes[29];
      boolean isPonds = biomeId == MFQM.AOABiomes[30];
      boolean isBubbles = biomeId == MFQM.AOABiomes[27];
      if (MFQM.GenMoss) {
         int MossP = 15;
         int genCount = 30 + random.nextInt(20);
         int genRad = 10;
         int genRadP = 10;
         if (!isRedForest && random.nextInt(MossP) == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX - 8 + random.nextInt(32);
               firstBlockYCoord = 67 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ - 8 + random.nextInt(32);
               int rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
               boolean canGen = false;

               for (int countBlock = 0; firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen) {
                  this.generateMoss(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenMoss && MFQM.GenBog && !blockGen) {
         boolean canGen = false;
         int genCount = 10 + random.nextInt(10);
         int genPos = 20;
         int genRad = 8;
         int genRadP = 6;
         int rad = 0;
         if (!isPonds && !isBubbles && !isRedForest && random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
            boolean Flat = false;
            if (random.nextInt(10) == 0) {
               Flat = true;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validSolidMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen
                  && this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad - 3, world)
                  && this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad - 3, world)) {
                  this.generateMossQS(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, MFQM.BogBlock);
                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateAOADimAbyss(World world, Random random, int chunkX, int chunkZ) {

                  world.func_147465_d(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, MFQM.MudBlock, 3, 0);
               }

         }

      if (MFQM.GenMeatS && !isDarkForest) {

                     && this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad + 1, world)
                     && this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad + 1, world)) {

                  }

      if (MFQM.GenLiqMire && !blockGen) {
