   private void generateAOADimDeeplands(World world, Random random, int chunkX, int chunkZ) {
      int dHeight = 0;
      int firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
      int firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
      int firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
      BiomeGenBase biomegenbase = world.func_72807_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
      int biomeId = biomegenbase.field_76756_M;
      boolean blockGen = false;
      if (MFQM.GenTar && !blockGen) {
         int tarP1 = 0;
         int tarP2 = 0;
         int tarPU = 0;
         boolean canGen = false;
         boolean genPosFounded = false;
         int genCount = 0;
         int genRealCount = 0;
         int genPos = 0;
         int genRad = 0;
         int genRadP = 0;
         int rad = 0;
         int FX = 0;
         int FZ = 0;
         int FY = 25;
         genCount = 20 + random.nextInt(10);
         int var48 = 10;
         int var49 = 5;
         int genSand = 0;
         genRealCount = 20 + random.nextInt(10);
         int radOfGen = (int)Math.floor(30.0 * Math.min(1.0, genRealCount / 20.0));
         int var47 = 25;
         int T_genRealCount = genRealCount * 3;
         Block aBlock = null;
         int aMeta = 0;
         boolean aDetBlock = false;
         if (random.nextInt(var47) == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCount && genRealCount > 0; i++) {
               if (!genPosFounded) {
                  firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(32) - 16;
                  firstBlockYCoord = 25 + dHeight;
                  firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(32) - 16;
                  rad = var48 + random.nextInt(var49);
               } else {
                  firstBlockXCoord = FX + random.nextInt(radOfGen) - radOfGen / 2;
                  firstBlockYCoord = 25 + dHeight;
                  firstBlockZCoord = FZ + random.nextInt(radOfGen) - radOfGen / 2;
                  rad = var48 - 2 + random.nextInt(5);
               }

               int countBlock = 0;
               int upper = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 18 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validTarMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        if (world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord) == MFQM.TarBlock) {
                           int var53 = -1;
                        } else {
                           int var54 = -1;
                        }
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen) {
                  canGen = false;
                  if (this.genIsFlatTar(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord - 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                     && this.genIsFlatTar(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                     && this.genIsFlatTar(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                     if (!aDetBlock) {
                        aBlock = world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
                        if (aBlock != null
                           && aBlock.func_149688_o().func_76220_a()
                           && aBlock.func_149662_c()
                           && !aBlock.func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                           && !aBlock.func_149716_u()) {
                           aMeta = world.func_72805_g(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
                           aDetBlock = true;
                        }
                     } else {
                        canGen = true;
                     }
                  }
               }

               if (canGen) {
                  if (!genPosFounded) {
                     genPosFounded = true;
                     FX = firstBlockXCoord;
                     FZ = firstBlockZCoord;
                     FY = 19;
                     genCount = 750;
                  }

                  genRealCount--;
                  this.generateCliffsTar(firstBlockXCoord, FY, firstBlockZCoord, (int)Math.floor(rad * 1.5), world, random, aBlock, aMeta);
                  this.generateFlatTar(firstBlockXCoord, FY, firstBlockZCoord, (int)Math.floor(rad * 1.5), world, random, genSand, aBlock, aMeta);
                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }

            if (aDetBlock) {
               int ccount = genSand * 10 + T_genRealCount + random.nextInt(T_genRealCount / 2);

               for (int i = 0; i < ccount; i++) {
                  firstBlockXCoord = FX + random.nextInt(radOfGen) - radOfGen / 2;
                  firstBlockZCoord = FZ + random.nextInt(radOfGen) - radOfGen / 2;
                  float disToC = Math.max((float)Math.sqrt(Math.pow(firstBlockXCoord - FX, 2.0) + Math.pow(firstBlockZCoord - FZ, 2.0)), 1.0F);
                  int cof_d = Math.max(1, (int)Math.floor(Math.pow(16.0F / disToC, 1.5)));
                  int rrad = 1;
                  if (random.nextInt(1 * cof_d) == 0) {
                     rrad = 2 + random.nextInt(1);
                  }

                  if (random.nextInt(2 * cof_d) == 0) {
                     rrad = 3 + random.nextInt(2);
                  }

                  if (random.nextInt(3 * cof_d) == 0) {
                     rrad = 4 + random.nextInt(3);
                  }

                  int qy = 1;
                  if (random.nextInt(5) == 0) {
                     qy = 0;
                  }

                  if (rrad > 1) {
                     this.generateRockTar(
                        firstBlockXCoord,
                        FY - qy - random.nextInt(Math.max(1, (int)Math.floor(rrad / 2))),
                        firstBlockZCoord,
                        rrad,
                        world,
                        random,
                        aBlock,
                        aMeta
                     );
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateAOADimHeaven(World world, Random random, int chunkX, int chunkZ) {
      int dHeight = 0;
      int firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
      int firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
      int firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
      BiomeGenBase biomegenbase = world.func_72807_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
      int biomeId = biomegenbase.field_76756_M;
      boolean blockGen = false;
      if (MFQM.GenHoney && !blockGen) {
         boolean canGen = false;
         int genType = 0;
         int genCount = 0;
         int genPos = 0;
         int genRad = 0;
         int genRadP = 0;
         int rad = 0;
         genCount = 40 + random.nextInt(20);
         int var29 = 7;
         int var30 = 13;
         int var28 = 7;
         boolean Flat = false;
         if (random.nextInt(5) == 0) {
            Flat = true;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(var28) == 0 && var28 != 999) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               rad = var29 + random.nextInt(var30);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 16 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validGroundMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen) {
                  if (this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                     && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                     if (Flat) {
                        this.generateMireO(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.HoneyBlock);
                     } else {
                        this.generateMireOD(
                           firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.HoneyBlock, 0
                        );
                     }

                     blockGen = true;
                  }
               } else if (firstBlockYCoord < 17 + dHeight) {
                  break;
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateTBL(World world, Random random, int chunkX, int chunkZ) {
      int dHeight = 18;
      int firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
      int firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
      int firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
      BiomeGenBase biomegenbase = world.func_72807_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
      int biomeId = biomegenbase.field_76756_M;
      boolean blockGen = false;
      boolean isSwamp = false;
      boolean isForest = false;
      boolean isDry = false;
      boolean isSnow = false;
      boolean isPlains = false;
      boolean isDark = false;
      if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.SWAMP)) {
         isSwamp = true;
      }

      if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.FOREST)) {
         isForest = true;
      }

      if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.FROZEN) || BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.COLD)) {
         isSnow = true;
      }

      if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.HOT)
         || BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.SAVANNA)
         || BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.DRY)) {
         isDry = true;
      }

      if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.PLAINS)) {
         isPlains = true;
      }

      if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.SPOOKY)) {
         isDark = true;
      }

      if (MFQM.GenSlime) {
         int genCount = 75 + random.nextInt(10);
         int realGen = 1;
         int genPos = 55;
         int genRad = 7;
         int genRadP = 10;
         int rad = 0;
         boolean canGen = false;
         if (random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCount && realGen > 0; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight - 50;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 16; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validSlimeMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validWaterMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen
                  && random.nextInt(firstBlockYCoord / 4) != 0
                  && this.genIsFlatSlime(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                  && this.genIsFlatSlime(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                  && this.genIsFlatSlime(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord - 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                  this.generateSlime(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random);
                  realGen--;
                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenWastePit) {
         int genCount = 125 + random.nextInt(10);
         int realGen = 2 + random.nextInt(3);
         int genPos = 5;
         int genRad = 7;
         int genRadP = 10;
         int rad = 0;
         boolean canGen = false;
         if (random.nextInt(genPos) == 0 && !blockGen) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCount && realGen > 0; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight - 50;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 20; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validSolidMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validWaterMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen
                  && random.nextInt(firstBlockYCoord / 10) == 0
                  && this.genIsFlatSlurry(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                  this.generateMireO(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.SlurryBlock);
                  realGen--;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      blockGen = false;
      if (MFQM.GenJQS && !blockGen) {
         boolean canGen = false;
         int genCount = 10 + random.nextInt(10);
         int genPos = 10;
         int genRad = 6;
         int genRadP = 10;
         int rad = 0;
         if (random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
            boolean Flat = false;
            if (random.nextInt(10) == 0) {
               Flat = true;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord) == MFQM.TarBlock) {
                     canGen = false;
                     break;
                  }

                  if (this.validGroundMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen
                  && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                  && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                  if (Flat) {
                     this.generateMireO(
                        firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.JungleQuicksandBlock
                     );
                  } else {
                     this.generateMireOD(
                        firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.JungleQuicksandBlock, 0
                     );
                  }

                  this.generateLeavesAround(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 3, firstBlockZCoord, rad + 3, world, random);
                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenLiqMire) {
         boolean canGen = false;
         int genType = 0;
         int genCount = 0;
         int genPos = 0;
         int genRad = 0;
         int genRadP = 0;
         int rad = 0;
         genCount = 5 + random.nextInt(10);
         int var72 = 7;
         int var80 = 5;
         int var67 = 4;
         if (random.nextInt(var67) == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight - 32;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               rad = (int)Math.floor(var72 * 1.5) + random.nextInt(var80);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 96 + dHeight - 36; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validWaterMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen && this.isGroundNear(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                  this.generateLiquidMire(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151586_h, 0);
                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenMoss && !blockGen) {
         int MossP = 65;
         int genCount = random.nextInt(5) + 10;
         int genRad = 10;
         int genRadP = 10;
         if (random.nextInt(MossP) == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight - 30;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               int rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
               int genType = 0;
               boolean canGen = false;

               for (int countBlock = 0; firstBlockYCoord > 96 + dHeight - 36; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen
                  && this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                  && this.isGroundMossNear(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                  this.generateMoss(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random);
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateEUDimDeepDark(World world, Random random, int chunkX, int chunkZ) {
      int dHeight = 0;
      int firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
      int firstBlockYCoord = 90 + dHeight;
      int firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
      BiomeGenBase biomegenbase = world.func_72807_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
      int biomeId = biomegenbase.field_76756_M;
      boolean blockGen = false;
      if (MFQM.GenMud && !blockGen) {
         boolean canGen = false;
         int genType = 0;
         int genCount = 0;
         int genPos = 0;
         int genRad = 0;
         int genRadP = 0;
         int rad = 0;
         genCount = 5 + random.nextInt(5);
         int var55 = 6;
         int var57 = 8;
         int var52 = 15;
         if (random.nextInt(var52) == 0 && var52 != 999) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 81 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               rad = var55 * 2 + random.nextInt(var57);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 75 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validSlimeMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     || this.validGroundOrLMireMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen) {
                  this.generateForcedMud(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, 0);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenTar && !blockGen) {
         int tarP1 = 0;
         int tarP2 = 0;
         int tarPU = 0;
         boolean canGen = false;
         boolean genPosFounded = false;
         int genCount = 0;
         int genRealCount = 0;
         int genPos = 0;
         int genRad = 0;
         int genRadP = 0;
         int rad = 0;
         int FX = 0;
         int FZ = 0;
         int FY = 25;
         genCount = 20 + random.nextInt(10);
         int var66 = 10;
         int var67 = 5;
         int genSand = 0;
         genRealCount = 20 + random.nextInt(10);
         int radOfGen = (int)Math.floor(30.0 * Math.min(1.0, genRealCount / 20.0));
         int var64 = 50;
         int T_genRealCount = genRealCount * 3;
         Block aBlock = null;
         int aMeta = 0;
         boolean aDetBlock = false;
         if (random.nextInt(var64) == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCount && genRealCount > 0; i++) {
               if (!genPosFounded) {
                  firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(32) - 16;
                  firstBlockYCoord = 90 + dHeight;
                  firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(32) - 16;
                  rad = var66 + random.nextInt(var67);
               } else {
                  firstBlockXCoord = FX + random.nextInt(radOfGen) - radOfGen / 2;
                  firstBlockYCoord = 90 + dHeight;
                  firstBlockZCoord = FZ + random.nextInt(radOfGen) - radOfGen / 2;
                  rad = var66 - 2 + random.nextInt(5);
               }

               int countBlock = 0;
               int upper = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 75 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validTarMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        if (world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord) == MFQM.TarBlock) {
                           int var71 = -1;
                        } else {
                           int var72 = -1;
                        }
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen) {
                  canGen = false;
                  if (this.genIsFlatTar(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord - 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                     && this.genIsFlatTar(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                     && this.genIsFlatTar(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                     if (!aDetBlock) {
                        aBlock = world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
                        if (aBlock != null
                           && aBlock != MFQM.MudBlock
                           && aBlock.func_149688_o().func_76220_a()
                           && aBlock.func_149662_c()
                           && !aBlock.func_149688_o().func_76224_d()
                           && !aBlock.func_149716_u()) {
                           aMeta = world.func_72805_g(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
                           aDetBlock = true;
                        }
                     } else {
                        canGen = true;
                     }
                  }
               }

               if (canGen) {
                  if (!genPosFounded) {
                     genPosFounded = true;
                     FX = firstBlockXCoord;
                     FZ = firstBlockZCoord;
                     FY = 81;
                     genCount = 750;
                  }

                  genRealCount--;
                  this.generateCliffsTar(firstBlockXCoord, FY, firstBlockZCoord, (int)Math.floor(rad * 1.5), world, random, aBlock, aMeta);
                  this.generateFlatTar(firstBlockXCoord, FY, firstBlockZCoord, (int)Math.floor(rad * 1.5), world, random, genSand, aBlock, aMeta);
                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }

            if (aDetBlock) {
               int ccount = genSand * 10 + T_genRealCount + random.nextInt(T_genRealCount / 2);

               for (int i = 0; i < ccount; i++) {
                  firstBlockXCoord = FX + random.nextInt(radOfGen) - radOfGen / 2;
                  firstBlockZCoord = FZ + random.nextInt(radOfGen) - radOfGen / 2;
                  float disToC = Math.max((float)Math.sqrt(Math.pow(firstBlockXCoord - FX, 2.0) + Math.pow(firstBlockZCoord - FZ, 2.0)), 1.0F);
                  int cof_d = Math.max(1, (int)Math.floor(Math.pow(16.0F / disToC, 1.5)));
                  int rrad = 1;
                  if (random.nextInt(1 * cof_d) == 0) {
                     rrad = 2 + random.nextInt(1);
                  }

                  if (random.nextInt(2 * cof_d) == 0) {
                     rrad = 3 + random.nextInt(2);
                  }

                  if (random.nextInt(3 * cof_d) == 0) {
                     rrad = 4 + random.nextInt(3);
                  }

                  int qy = 1;
                  if (random.nextInt(5) == 0) {
                     qy = 0;
                  }

                  if (rrad > 1) {
                     this.generateRockTar(
                        firstBlockXCoord,
                        FY - qy - random.nextInt(Math.max(1, (int)Math.floor(rrad / 2))),
                        firstBlockZCoord,
                        rrad,
                        world,
                        random,
                        aBlock,
                        aMeta
                     );
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateWLD(World world, Random random, int chunkX, int chunkZ) {
      int dHeight = 0;
      int firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + 7;
      int firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
      int firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + 7;
      BiomeGenBase biomegenbase = world.func_72807_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
      int biomeId = biomegenbase.field_76756_M;
      boolean blockGen = false;
      boolean isForest = biomeId == MFQM.WLDBiomes[0];
      boolean isDesert = biomeId == MFQM.WLDBiomes[1];
      boolean isSnow = biomeId == MFQM.WLDBiomes[2];
      boolean isTained = biomeId == MFQM.WLDBiomes[3];
      boolean isAshes = biomeId == MFQM.WLDBiomes[4];
      boolean isVolcan = biomeId == MFQM.WLDBiomes[5];
      if (MFQM.GenQS && !blockGen && isDesert) {
         boolean canGen = false;
         int genCount = 2 + random.nextInt(5);
         int genPos = 10;
         int genRad = 5;
         int genRadP = 7;
         int rad = 0;
         if (random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validSandMaterial(false, firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen
                  && this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                  && this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                  this.generateMire(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.QuicksandBlock);
                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }

         canGen = false;

         for (int hc = -3; hc <= 3 && !canGen; hc++) {
            for (int vc = -3; vc <= 3 && !canGen; vc++) {
               if (world.func_72899_e(chunkX + 8 + hc * 16, 32, chunkZ + 8 + vc * 16)) {
                  for (int k = 0; k < 16 && !canGen; k++) {
                     for (int h = 0; h < 16 && !canGen; h++) {
                        for (int v = 0; v < 16 && !canGen; v++) {
                           firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + hc * 16 + h;
                           firstBlockYCoord = 63 + dHeight + k;
                           firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + vc * 16 + v;
                           if (world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151587_i) {
                              canGen = true;
                              break;
                           }
                        }
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }

         if (canGen) {
            canGen = false;
            int bottomPyr = 0;
            int XPyr = firstBlockXCoord;
            int ZPyr = firstBlockZCoord;
            int YPyr = firstBlockYCoord;

            for (int count = 5; count > 0 && !canGen; count--) {
               int xx = random.nextInt(16) - 8;
               int zz = random.nextInt(16) - 8;

               for (int var59 = YPyr; var59 > 60 + dHeight; var59--) {
                  if (world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord + xx, var59, firstBlockZCoord + zz).func_149688_o() == Material.field_151595_p) {
                     canGen = true;
                     bottomPyr = var59;
                     break;
                  }
               }
            }

            if (canGen) {
               int puddleCount = -2;

               for (int k = 20; k >= 0; k--) {
                  for (int h = -35 + k; h < 35 - k; h++) {
                     for (int vx = -35 + k; vx < 35 - k; vx++) {
                        firstBlockXCoord = XPyr + h;
                        firstBlockYCoord = bottomPyr + k;
                        firstBlockZCoord = ZPyr + vx;
                        if (world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord) == Blocks.field_150474_ac && random.nextInt(2) == 0) {
                           world.func_147449_b(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, Blocks.field_150402_ci);
                           if (random.nextInt(4) == 0) {
                              world.func_147449_b(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, Blocks.field_150368_y);
                           } else if (random.nextInt(8) == 0) {
                              world.func_147449_b(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, Blocks.field_150451_bX);
                           } else if (random.nextInt(16) == 0) {
                              world.func_147449_b(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, Blocks.field_150340_R);
                           } else if (random.nextInt(32) == 0) {
                              world.func_147449_b(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, Blocks.field_150339_S);
                           } else if (random.nextInt(64) == 0) {
                              world.func_147449_b(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, Blocks.field_150484_ah);
                           } else if (random.nextInt(128) == 0) {
                              world.func_147449_b(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, Blocks.field_150475_bE);

                     world.func_147449_b(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord - depth, firstBlockZCoord, Blocks.field_150348_b);

                     depth--;
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

                     && this.genIsFlatTar(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord - 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                     && this.genIsFlatTar(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                     && this.genIsFlatTar(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                     if (!genPosFounded) {
                        genPosFounded = true;
                        FX = firstBlockXCoord;
                        FZ = firstBlockZCoord;
                        FY = firstBlockYCoord;
                        genCount = 750;
                     }

                     genRealCount--;
                     this.generateCliffsTar(firstBlockXCoord, FY, firstBlockZCoord, (int)Math.floor(rad * 1.5), world, random, aBlock, aMeta);
                     this.generateFlatTar(firstBlockXCoord, FY - upper - 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, genSand, aBlock, aMeta);
                     blockGen = true;
                  }
               }

               if (aDetBlock) {
                  int ccount = genSand * 10 + T_genRealCount + random.nextInt(T_genRealCount / 2);
                  int rcount = 0;

                  for (int i = 0; rcount < ccount; i++) {
                     firstBlockXCoord = FX + random.nextInt(radOfGen) - radOfGen / 2;
                     firstBlockZCoord = FZ + random.nextInt(radOfGen) - radOfGen / 2;
                     float disToC = Math.max((float)Math.sqrt(Math.pow(firstBlockXCoord - FX, 2.0) + Math.pow(firstBlockZCoord - FZ, 2.0)), 1.0F);
                     int cof_d = Math.max(1, (int)Math.floor(Math.pow(16.0F / disToC, 1.5)));
                     int rrad = 1;
                     if (random.nextInt(1 * cof_d) == 0) {
                        rrad = 2 + random.nextInt(1);
                     }

                     if (random.nextInt(2 * cof_d) == 0) {
                        rrad = 3 + random.nextInt(2);
                     }

                     if (random.nextInt(3 * cof_d) == 0) {
                        rrad = 4 + random.nextInt(3);
                     }

                     int qy = 1;
                     if (random.nextInt(5) == 0) {
                        qy = 0;
                     }

                     if (rrad > 1) {
                        this.generateRockTar(
                           firstBlockXCoord,
                           FY - qy - random.nextInt(Math.max(1, (int)Math.floor(rrad / 2))),
                           firstBlockZCoord,
                           rrad,
                           world,
                           random,
                           aBlock,
                           aMeta
                        );
                     }

                     rcount++;
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateABC1(World world, Random random, int chunkX, int chunkZ) {
      int dHeight = 0;
      int firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
      int firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
      int firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
      BiomeGenBase biomegenbase = world.func_72807_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
      int biomeId = biomegenbase.field_76756_M;
      boolean blockGen = false;
      if (MFQM.GenMud && !blockGen) {
         boolean canGen = false;
         int genType = 0;
         int genCount = 0;
         int genPos = 0;
         int genRad = 0;
         int genRadP = 0;
         int rad = 0;
         genCount = 10 + random.nextInt(10);
         int var45 = 6;
         int var49 = 7;
         int var41 = 5;
         if (random.nextInt(var41) == 0 && var41 != 999) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               rad = var45 * 3 + random.nextInt(var49);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 55 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validSlimeMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     || this.validGroundOrLMireMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen) {
                  this.generateForcedMud(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 3, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, 0);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenSlime) {
         boolean canGen = false;
         int genCount = 5 + random.nextInt(2);
         int genPos = 10;
         int genRad = 6;
         int genRadP = 10;
         int rad = 0;
         if (random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
            boolean Flat = false;
            if (random.nextInt(10) == 0) {
               Flat = true;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord) == MFQM.TarBlock) {
                     canGen = false;
                     break;
                  }

                  if ((
                        this.validSlimeMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                           || this.validGroundOrLMireMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     )
                     && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen
                  && this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                  && this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                  if (!Flat && this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad * 2, world)) {
                     this.generateMireOD(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.SlimeBlock, 0);
                  } else {
                     this.generateMireO(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.SlimeBlock);
                  }

                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenTar) {
         int trType = 0;
         int tarP1 = 0;
         int tarP2 = 0;
         int tarPU = 0;
         int var36 = 2;
         int var40 = 35;
         int var44 = 20;
         if (random.nextInt(var36) == 0 && random.nextInt(var40) == 0) {
            int var5 = chunkX + random.nextInt(16) + 8;
            int var6 = 60 + random.nextInt(21) + dHeight;
            int var7 = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16) + 8;
            new CustomWorldGenLakes(MFQM.TarBlock).generate(world, random, var5, var6, var7, 1);
            blockGen = true;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(var36) == 0 && random.nextInt(var44) == 0) {
            int var5 = chunkX + random.nextInt(16) + 8;
            int var6 = random.nextInt(64) + dHeight;
            int var7 = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16) + 8;
            new CustomWorldGenLakes(MFQM.TarBlock).generate(world, random, var5, var6, var7, 1);
            blockGen = true;
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateCustom(World world, Random random, int chunkX, int chunkZ, int height) {
      float MireMul = 1.0F;
      if (world.field_73011_w.field_76574_g == MFQM.TFDim) {
         MireMul = 10.0F;
      }

      int dHeight = height - 62;
      float wordSizer = Math.max(height / 62.0F, 1.0F);
      int firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
      int firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
      int firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
      BiomeGenBase biomegenbase = world.func_72807_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
      int biomeId = biomegenbase.field_76756_M;
      boolean blockGen = false;
      boolean isSwamp = false;
      boolean isForest = false;
      boolean isBeach = false;
      boolean isDesert = false;
      boolean isJungle = false;
      boolean isDry = false;
      boolean isWet = false;
      boolean isSnow = false;
      boolean isPlains = false;
      boolean isDark = false;
      int swampType = 0;
      if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.SWAMP)) {
         isSwamp = true;
         if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.RIVER)) {
            isSwamp = false;
         }

         if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.OCEAN)) {
            isSwamp = false;
         }
      }

      if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.FOREST)) {
         isForest = true;
      }

      if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.FROZEN) || BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.COLD)) {
         isSnow = true;
      }

      if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.SPOOKY)) {
         isDark = true;
      }

      if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.HOT)
         || BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.SAVANNA)
         || BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.DRY)) {
         isDry = true;
      }

      if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.WET)) {
         isWet = true;
      }

      if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.PLAINS)) {
         isPlains = true;
      }

      if (world.field_73011_w.field_76574_g != MFQM.TFDim) {
         if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.JUNGLE)) {
            isJungle = true;
         }

         if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.BEACH)) {
            isBeach = true;
         }

         if (BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.DESERT) || BiomeDictionary.isBiomeOfType(biomegenbase, Type.SANDY)) {
            isDesert = true;
         }
      }

      double altitude = Math.cos(chunkZ * Math.PI / 180.0 / 333.333);
      if (isSwamp) {
         swampType = 0;
         if (MireMul < 10.0F) {
            int regionX = (int)Math.floor(chunkX / 256.0) * 256;
            int regionZ = (int)Math.floor(chunkZ / 256.0) * 256;
            swampType = 0;
            if ((regionX * regionX + regionZ) % 2 == 0) {
               if (altitude > 0.8) {
                  swampType = 2;
               } else {
                  swampType = 4;
               }
            }

            if ((regionX * regionX + regionZ) % 5 == 0) {
               if (altitude > 0.9) {
                  swampType = 3;
               } else {
                  swampType = 4;
               }
            }

            if ((regionX * regionX * regionX + regionZ * regionZ * regionZ) % 7 == 0) {
               swampType = 0;
            }

            if (altitude < 0.5) {
               isSwamp = false;
            }
         }
      }

      if (MireMul < 10.0F) {
         if (altitude < 0.5) {
            isSnow = true;
            isBeach = false;
            isDesert = false;
            isJungle = false;
            isForest = false;
         } else if (altitude >= 0.5) {
            if (altitude > 0.95) {
               isJungle = true;
            } else {
               isDesert = !isSwamp && !isBeach;
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenQS && !blockGen && (isDesert || isBeach)) {
         boolean canGen = false;
         int genCount = 0;
         int genPos = 0;
         int genRad = 0;
         int genRadP = 0;
         boolean sandOnly = false;
         int rad = 0;
         if (isDesert) {
            genCount = 10 + random.nextInt(10);
            genRad = 5;
            sandOnly = true;
            genRadP = 10;
            genPos = 40;
         }

         if (isBeach) {
            genCount = 10 + random.nextInt(10);
            genRad = 5;
            sandOnly = true;
            genRadP = 5;
            genPos = 15;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validSandMaterial(sandOnly, firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen
                  && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                  && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                  this.generateMire(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.QuicksandBlock);
                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenJQS && !blockGen && isJungle) {
         boolean canGenx = false;
         boolean canForceGen = false;
         int genCountx = 10 + random.nextInt(10);
         int genPosx = 10;
         if (MireMul < 10.0F) {
            genPosx = (int)Math.floor(20.0 / Math.pow(altitude + 0.001, 4.0));
         }

         int genRadx = 6;
         int genRadPx = 10;
         int radx = 0;
         if (random.nextInt(genPosx) == 0) {
            boolean Flat = false;
            if (random.nextInt(10) == 0) {
               Flat = true;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < genCountx; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               radx = genRadx + random.nextInt(genRadPx);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGenx = false; firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord) == MFQM.TarBlock) {
                     canGenx = false;
                     break;
                  }

                  if (this.validGroundMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGenx = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGenx) {
                  if (!canForceGen && this.isNearAbovePlant(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world, radx)) {
                     canForceGen = true;
                  }

                  if (this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, radx, world)
                     && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, radx, world)) {
                     if (isForest && !isDry) {
                        if (Flat) {
                           this.generateMireO(
                              firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, radx, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.JungleQuicksandBlock
                           );
                        } else {
                           this.generateMireOD(
                              firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, radx, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.JungleQuicksandBlock, 0
                           );
                        }

                        this.generateLeavesAround(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 3, firstBlockZCoord, radx + 3, world, random);
                        blockGen = true;
                     } else if (canForceGen) {
                        if (Flat) {
                           this.generateMireO(
                              firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, radx, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.JungleQuicksandBlock
                           );
                        } else {
                           this.generateMireOD(
                              firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, radx, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.JungleQuicksandBlock, 0
                           );
                        }

                        this.generateLeavesAround(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 3, firstBlockZCoord, radx + 3, world, random);
                        blockGen = true;
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenSSnow && !blockGen && isSnow) {
         boolean canGenxx = false;
         int genCountxx = 0;
         int genPosxx = 0;
         int genRadx = 0;
         int genRadPx = 0;
         int radx = 0;
         genCountxx = 20 + random.nextInt(10);
         int var176 = 5;
         int var198 = 10;
         int var153 = 60;
         if (random.nextInt(var153) == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCountxx; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX - 16 + random.nextInt(32);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ - 16 + random.nextInt(32);
               radx = var176 + random.nextInt(var198);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGenxx = false; firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validSnowMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGenxx = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGenxx
                  && this.genIsFlatSnow(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, radx, world)
                  && this.genIsFlatSnow(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, radx, world)) {
                  this.generateSnow(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord - 1, firstBlockZCoord, radx, world, random);
                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenSQSF && !blockGen && (!isDesert && !isSnow || isForest && !isDry)) {
         boolean canGenxx = false;
         boolean canForceGenx = false;
         int genCountxx = 0;
         int genPosxx = 0;
         int genRadx = 0;
         int genRadPx = 0;
         int radx = 0;
         genCountxx = 5 + random.nextInt(10);
         int var200 = 5;
         int var220 = 6;
         short var178;
         if (MireMul > 5.0F) {
            var178 = 175;
         } else {
            var178 = 80;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(var178) == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCountxx; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               radx = var200 + random.nextInt(var220);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGenxx = false; firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validGroundMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGenxx = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGenxx) {
                  if (!canForceGenx && this.isNearAbovePlant(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world, radx)) {
                     canForceGenx = true;
                  }

                  if ((
                        MireMul <= 5.0F && this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, radx, world)
                           || this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, radx, world)
                     )
                     && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, radx, world)) {
                     if (isForest && !isDry) {
                        this.generateSoftQuicksandForest(
                           firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, radx, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                        );
                        blockGen = true;
                     } else if (canForceGenx) {
                        this.generateSoftQuicksandForest(
                           firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, radx, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.SoftQuicksandBlock
                        );
                        blockGen = true;
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenWastePit && !blockGen && isSwamp && swampType == 3) {
         boolean canGenxxx = false;
         int genCountxxx = 50 + random.nextInt(50);
         int genPosxxx = 10;
         int genRadxx = 10;
         int genRadPxx = 10;
         int radxx = 0;
         if (random.nextInt(genPosxxx) == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCountxxx; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               radxx = genRadxx + random.nextInt(genRadPxx);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGenxxx = false; firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord) == MFQM.TarBlock) {
                     canGenxxx = false;
                     break;
                  }

                  if (this.validGroundMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGenxxx = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGenxxx
                  && this.genIsFlatSlurry(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, radxx, world)
                  && this.genIsFlatSlurry(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, radxx, world)) {
                  this.generateMud(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 3, firstBlockZCoord, (int)Math.floor(radxx * 1.75), world, random);
                  if (random.nextInt(10) == 0) {
                     this.generateMireD(
                        firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, radxx, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.SlurryBlock, true
                     );
                  } else {
                     this.generateMire(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, radxx, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.SlurryBlock);
                  }

                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }

         if (MFQM.GenMud && random.nextInt(genPosxxx * 2) == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCountxxx; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               radxx = genRadxx * 3 + random.nextInt(genRadPxx);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGenxxx = false; firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validGroundOrLMireMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGenxxx = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 10) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGenxxx) {
                  this.generateMud(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 3, firstBlockZCoord, radxx, world, random);
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenMorass && !blockGen) {
         int BogP = 0;
         int genCountxxxx = 0;
         int trueGenCount = 0;
         int genRadxxx = 0;
         int trueGenRad = 0;
         float dynamicGenRad = 0.0F;
         int genRadPxxx = 0;
         int swD = this.getPeatBogType(biomeId);
         int GPX = 0;
         int GPY = 0;
         int GPZ = 0;
         int BGPX = 0;
