         byte var195;
         if (isDark) {
            var150 = 10;
            var173 = 8;
            var195 = 10;
         } else {
            var150 = 35;
            var173 = 15;
            var195 = 10;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(var150) == 0 && random.nextInt(var173) == 0) {
            int var5x = chunkX + random.nextInt(16) + 8;
            int var6 = 60 + random.nextInt(21) + dHeight;
            int var7 = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16) + 8;
            new CustomWorldGenLakes(MFQM.TarBlock).generate(world, random, var5x, var6, var7, 1);
            blockGen = true;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(var150) == 0 && random.nextInt(var195) == 0) {
            int var5x = chunkX + random.nextInt(16) + 8;
            int var6 = random.nextInt(64) + dHeight;
            int var7 = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16) + 8;
            new CustomWorldGenLakes(MFQM.TarBlock).generate(world, random, var5x, var6, var7, 1);
            blockGen = true;
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateNether(World world, Random random, int chunkX, int chunkZ) {
      this.T_BoP = MFQM.BoP;
      if (MFQM.PBoP
         && MFQM.BoP
         && (world.func_72912_H().func_76067_t() == WorldType.field_77135_d || world.func_72912_H().func_76067_t() == WorldType.field_77137_b)) {
         this.T_BoP = false;
      }

      int firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
      int firstBlockYCoord = 96;
      int firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
      BiomeGenBase biomegenbase = world.func_72807_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
      int biomeId = biomegenbase.field_76756_M;
      boolean blockGen = false;
      if (MFQM.GenTar) {
         int trType = 0;
         int tarP1 = 0;
         int tarP2 = 0;
         int tarPU = 0;
         trType = this.getTarCount(biomeId);
         if (MFQM.BoP && trType == 0) {
            trType = this.isTarBiomeIsNear(chunkX + 8, 64, chunkZ + 8, world);
         }

         byte var45;
         byte var52;
         switch (trType) {
            case 4:
               var45 = 1;
               var52 = 3;
               int var56 = 3;
               break;
            default:
               var45 = 8;
               var52 = 5;
               int var55 = 5;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(var45) == 0 && random.nextInt(var52) == 0) {
            int var5 = chunkX + random.nextInt(16) + 8;
            int var6 = random.nextInt(128);
            int var7 = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16) + 8;
            new CustomWorldGenLakes(MFQM.TarBlock).generate(world, random, var5, var6, var7, 0);
            blockGen = true;
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenCSand && !blockGen) {
         int dT = this.getCSandsType(biomeId);
         boolean canGen = false;
         int genCount = 0;
         int genPos = 0;
         int genRad = 0;
         int genRadP = 0;
         int rad = 0;
         switch (dT) {
            case 0:
               genCount = 10 + random.nextInt(10);
               genRad = 7;
               genRadP = 7;
               genPos = 5;
               break;
            case 1:
               genCount = 20 + random.nextInt(20);
               genRad = 7;
               genRadP = 7;
               genPos = 5;
         }

         if (random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
            for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 30 + random.nextInt(98);
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 30; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validCSMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validLavaMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (countBlock > 50) {
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen
                  && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                  && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                  this.generateMire(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151595_p, MFQM.CSandBlock);
                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if ((MFQM.GenMeatS || MFQM.GenMeat || MFQM.GenMeatAss) && !blockGen) {
         int dT = this.getBloodType(biomeId);
         boolean canGen = false;
         int genCount = 0;
         int genPos = 0;
         int genRad = 0;
         int genRadP = 0;
         int rad = 0;
         switch (dT) {
            case 0:
               genCount = 30 + random.nextInt(10);
               genRad = 10;
               genRadP = 7;
               genPos = 50;
               break;
            case 1:
               genCount = 40 + random.nextInt(20);
               genRad = 10;
               genRadP = 7;
               genPos = 4;
         }

         if (dT == 0 && this.T_BoP) {
            genPos *= 100;
         }

         if (!this.T_BoP) {
            double POX = chunkX / 20.0;
            double POZ = chunkZ / 20.0;
            double srfX = 1.1 * Math.sin(POX * 0.2623) + 3.2 * Math.sin(POX * 0.9006);
            double srfZ = 1.1 * Math.sin(POZ * 0.7623) - 3.0 * Math.cos(POZ * 0.2006) + 2.0 * Math.sin(POX * 0.0106);
            if (random.nextInt((int)Math.floor(Math.max(Math.abs(srfX * 10.0 + srfZ * 10.0), 1.0))) == 0) {
               genPos = 1;
            }
         }

         boolean genHole = false;
         if (random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
            if (MFQM.GenMeatS) {
               for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
                  firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
                  firstBlockYCoord = 40 + random.nextInt(98);
                  firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
                  rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
                  int countBlock = 0;

                  for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 40; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                     if ((
                           this.validBloodMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                              || this.validVoreMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                        )
                        && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                           canGen = true;
                           break;
                        }

                        countBlock++;
                     }

                     if (countBlock > 50) {
                        break;
                     }
                  }

                  if (canGen) {
                     this.generateBlood(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad * 2, world, random, Material.field_151568_F);
                     this.generateBlood(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad * 2, world, random, Material.field_151568_F);
                     this.generateBlood(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad * 2, world, random, Material.field_151568_F);
                     if ((!MFQM.GenMeat || random.nextInt(5) != 0) && MFQM.GenMeat) {
                        genHole = true;
                     } else if (this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad + 1, world)
                        && this.genIsFlatSolid(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad + 1, world)) {
                        this.generateMire2(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.SwFleshBlock);
                        this.generateTendrils(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 5, firstBlockZCoord, rad * 2, world, random);
                        genHole = false;
                        blockGen = true;
                     }
                  }
               }
            }

            if (MFQM.GenMeat) {
               boolean blockGen2 = false;
               if (genHole) {
                  for (int i = 0; i < genCount * 2 && !blockGen2; i++) {
                     firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(12) + 2;
                     firstBlockYCoord = 40 + random.nextInt(98);
                     firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(12) + 2;
                     rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
                     int countBlock = 0;

                     for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 40; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                        if (this.validVoreMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                           && !this.validLavaMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                           if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                              if (this.isVorePlace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                                 canGen = true;
                                 break;
                              }
                           } else {
                              countBlock++;
                           }
                        }

                        if (countBlock > 50) {
                           break;
                        }
                     }

                     if (canGen && this.genIsFlatVore(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, 6, world)) {
                        this.generateVore(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world, random);
                        blockGen2 = true;
                     }
                  }
               }
            }

            if (MFQM.GenMeatAss) {
               int assGen = 0;
               int BlockYCoord = 0;
               int BlockXCoord = 0;
               int BlockZCoord = 0;
               int realGen = genCount / 4;
               if (random.nextInt(genPos + 5) == 0) {
                  for (int i = 0; i < genCount * 40 && realGen > 0; i++) {
                     if (BlockYCoord == 0) {
                        firstBlockYCoord = 20 + random.nextInt(30);
                        firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
                        firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
                     } else {
                        firstBlockYCoord = BlockYCoord + 1;
                        firstBlockXCoord = BlockXCoord + random.nextInt(11) - 5;
                        firstBlockZCoord = BlockZCoord + random.nextInt(11) - 5;
                     }

                     rad = genRad - 2 + random.nextInt(genRadP);
                     int countBlock = 0;

                     for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 30; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                        if ((
                              this.validBloodMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                                 || this.validVoreMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                                 || this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                                    && world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord) != MFQM.AcidBlock
                                    && world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord).func_149688_o() != Material.field_151587_i
                           )
                           && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                           && !isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                           if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                              canGen = true;
                              break;
                           }

                           countBlock++;
                        }

                        if (countBlock > 50 || BlockYCoord != 0 && firstBlockYCoord < BlockYCoord - 1) {
                           break;
                        }
                     }

                     if (canGen) {
                        assGen -= this.ClearArea2(
                           firstBlockXCoord + random.nextInt(9) - 4, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord + random.nextInt(9) - 4, rad + 1, world, random
                        );
                        if (assGen < 0) {
                           assGen = 0;
                        }

                        this.generateBlood2(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad + 5, world, random);
                        this.generateBlood2(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 2, firstBlockZCoord, rad + 5, world, random);
                        this.generateBlood2(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 3, firstBlockZCoord, rad + 5, world, random);
                        firstBlockXCoord += random.nextInt(9) - 4;
                        firstBlockZCoord += random.nextInt(9) - 4;
                        if (this.genIsFlatSlurry(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad + 1, world)
                           && this.genIsFlatSlurry(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad + 1, world)) {
                           this.generateMud2(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, (int)Math.floor(rad * 1.75), world, random);
                           this.generateSlurry(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random);
                           BlockYCoord = firstBlockYCoord;
                           BlockZCoord = firstBlockZCoord;
                           BlockXCoord = firstBlockXCoord;
                           if (random.nextInt(assGen * 2 + 1) == 0
                              && this.generateAss(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, 5 + random.nextInt(5), world, random)) {
                              assGen++;
                           }

                           realGen--;
                           blockGen = true;
                        }
                     }
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }
   }

   private void generateSurface(World world, Random random, int chunkX, int chunkZ, int height) {
      this.T_BoP = MFQM.BoP;
      int dHeight = height - 62;
      float wordSizer = height / 62.0F;
      if (MFQM.PBoP
         && MFQM.BoP
         && (world.func_72912_H().func_76067_t() == WorldType.field_77135_d || world.func_72912_H().func_76067_t() == WorldType.field_77137_b)) {
         this.T_BoP = false;
      }

      int firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
      int firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
      int firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
      BiomeGenBase biomegenbase = world.func_72807_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
      int biomeId = biomegenbase.field_76756_M;
      boolean blockGen = false;
      if (MFQM.GenDesertTombs && !blockGen) {
         int dT = this.getDesertType(biomeId);
         if ((dT == 1 || dT == 6) && this.T_BoP || biomegenbase instanceof BiomeGenDesert && !this.T_BoP) {
            boolean canGen = false;
            int genCount = 0;
            int genPos = 0;
            int rad = 0;
            int var126 = true;
            int var165 = 750;
            if (var165 > 0 && random.nextFloat() * var165 > var165 - 1) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 70 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validSolidMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     canGen = true;
                     break;
                  }
               }

               if (canGen) {
                  new GenerateDesertTombs().func_76484_a(world, random, firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord - 8 - random.nextInt(9), firstBlockZCoord);
                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenWastePit && !blockGen) {
         int slrT = this.getSlurryType(biomeId);
         boolean dep = false;
         if (random.nextInt(10) == 0) {
            dep = true;
         }

         if (slrT != 0 && this.T_BoP) {
            boolean canGen = false;
            int genCount = 30 + random.nextInt(30);
            int genPos = 75;
            int genRad = 10;
            int genRadP = 10;
            int rad = 0;
            if (random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
               for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
                  firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
                  firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
                  firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
                  rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
                  int countBlock = 0;

                  for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                     if (world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord) == MFQM.TarBlock) {
                        canGen = false;
                        break;
                     }

                     if (this.validSolidMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                        && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                           canGen = true;
                           break;
                        }

                        countBlock++;
                     }

                     if (countBlock > 10) {
                        break;
                     }
                  }

                  if (canGen
                     && this.genIsFlatSlurry(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                     && this.genIsFlatSlurry(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                     if (dep) {
                        this.generateMireD(
                           firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.SlurryBlock, true
                        );
                     } else {
                        this.generateMire(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random, Material.field_151568_F, MFQM.SlurryBlock);
                     }

                     blockGen = true;
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenWax && !blockGen) {
         int jngT = this.getJungleType(biomeId);
         if (jngT != 0 && this.T_BoP || biomegenbase instanceof BiomeGenJungle && !this.T_BoP) {
            boolean canGen = false;
            int genCount = 52;
            int genPos = 85;
            int genRad = 16;
            if ((jngT == 3 || jngT == 0) && random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
               for (int i = 0; i < genCount; i++) {
                  firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
                  firstBlockYCoord = 75 - random.nextInt(8) + dHeight;
                  firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
                  if (this.generateWaxTree(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, genRad, world, random)) {
                     blockGen = true;
                     break;
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenHoney && !blockGen) {
         int jngT = this.getJungleType(biomeId);
         if (jngT != 0 && this.T_BoP || biomegenbase instanceof BiomeGenJungle && !this.T_BoP) {
            boolean canGen = false;
            int genCount = 50;
            int realGen = 0;
            int genPos = 175;
            int genRad = 16;
            int genRadP = 16;
            int rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
            int arad = 16 + random.nextInt(10);
            if (jngT != 2 && random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
               for (int ix = 0; ix < genCount; ix++) {
                  firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(32) - 16;
                  firstBlockYCoord = 80 - random.nextInt(8) + dHeight;
                  firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(32) - 16;
                  int countBlock = 0;

                  for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                     if (this.validSolidMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        canGen = true;
                        break;
                     }

                     countBlock++;
                  }

                  if (canGen) {
                     if (!this.genIsHoneycombNear(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, arad * 2, world)) {
                        int ahoney = 20;
                        this.generateHoneycomb(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, arad, world, random, ahoney);
                        this.generateHoneyNest(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random);
                        blockGen = true;
                     }
                     break;
                  }
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenSlime) {
         int genCount = 75 + random.nextInt(10);
         int realGen = 1;
         int genPos = 75;
         int genRad = 7;
         int genRadP = 10;
         int rad = 0;
         boolean canGen = false;
         if (random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
            for (int ix = 0; ix < genCount && realGen > 0; ix++) {
               firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(16);
               firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
               firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(16);
               rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
               int countBlock = 0;

               for (canGen = false; firstBlockYCoord > 16; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                  if (this.validSlimeMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                     && !this.validWaterMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                     if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        if (this.isInCave(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world, rad)) {
                           canGen = true;
                           break;
                        }
                     } else {
                        countBlock++;
                     }
                  }
               }

               if (canGen
                  && random.nextInt((int)Math.floor(firstBlockYCoord / 4 / wordSizer)) != 0
                  && this.genIsFlatSlime(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                  && this.genIsFlatSlime(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                  && this.genIsFlatSlime(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord - 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)) {
                  this.generateSlime(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world, random);
                  realGen--;
                  blockGen = true;
               }
            }
         }
      }

      if (MFQM.GenWeb && !blockGen) {
         int sT = this.getLarvType(biomeId);
         if (sT != 0 && this.T_BoP || biomeId == MFQM.LarvBiomes[2] && !this.T_BoP) {
            boolean canGen = false;
            boolean genPosFounded = false;
            int genCount = 0;
            int genRealCount = 0;
            int genPos = 0;
            int genRad = 0;
            int genRadP = 0;
            int rad = 0;
            int FX = 0;
            int FZ = 0;
            switch (sT) {
               case 0:
                  genCount = 20 + random.nextInt(10);
                  genRad = 7;
                  genRadP = 10;
                  genPos = 125;
                  break;
               case 1:
                  genCount = 20 + random.nextInt(10);
                  genRad = 7;
                  genRadP = 10;
                  genPos = 100;
                  break;
               case 2:
                  genCount = 20 + random.nextInt(10);
                  genRad = 7;
                  genRadP = 10;
                  genPos = 75;
                  break;
               case 3:
                  genCount = 20 + random.nextInt(10);
                  genRad = 7;
                  genRadP = 10;
                  genPos = 125;
            }

            genRealCount = 15 + random.nextInt(10);
            if (random.nextInt(genPos) == 0) {
               for (int ix = 0; ix < genCount && genRealCount > 0; ix++) {
                  if (!genPosFounded) {
                     firstBlockXCoord = chunkX + random.nextInt(32) - 16;
                     firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
                     firstBlockZCoord = chunkZ + random.nextInt(32) - 16;
                     rad = genRad + random.nextInt(genRadP);
                  } else {
                     firstBlockXCoord = FX + random.nextInt(24) - 12;
                     firstBlockYCoord = 96 + dHeight;
                     firstBlockZCoord = FZ + random.nextInt(24) - 12;
                     rad = genRad + random.nextInt(10);
                  }

                  int countBlock = 0;
                  int upper = 0;
                  canGen = false;
                  biomegenbase = world.func_72807_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockZCoord);
                  biomeId = biomegenbase.field_76756_M;

                  for (int var61 = this.getLarvType(biomeId);
                     firstBlockYCoord > 60 + dHeight && (var61 != 0 && this.T_BoP || biomeId == MFQM.LarvBiomes[2] && !this.T_BoP);
                     firstBlockYCoord--
                  ) {
                     if (this.validGroundMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)
                        && !this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                           canGen = true;
                           if (world.func_147439_a(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord) == MFQM.DenseWebBlock) {
                              upper = 0;
                           } else {
                              upper = 1;
                           }
                           break;
                        }

                        countBlock++;
                     }
                  }

                  if (canGen
                     && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                     && this.genIsFlat(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, rad, world)
                     && (genPosFounded || this.isNearAboveCeil(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world, rad))) {

                  if (isSinking || random.nextInt(Math.max(1, firstBlockYCoord - 64) * notGen) == 0) {
                     this.generateOnlyClay(isSinking, firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, (int)Math.floor(radV * 1.5F), world, random);
                     TacktGen = true;
                     PXC = firstBlockXCoord;
                     PYC = firstBlockYCoord + 2;
                     PZC = firstBlockZCoord;
                     if (PAC == 0) {
                        genCountxxxx = 40 + random.nextInt(10);
                        PAC = 1;
                     }

                     blockGen = true;
                  }
               }

               if (!TacktGen) {
                  firstBlockXCoord = XC;
                  firstBlockYCoord = YC;
                  firstBlockZCoord = ZC;
                  radxxxxxx = 4 + random.nextInt(1);
                  int var500 = 0;
                  countBlock2 = 0;

                  for (canGenxxxxxxx = false; firstBlockYCoord > 60; firstBlockYCoord--) {
                     if (this.validGroundClayMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                        if (isAboveFreeSpace(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                           if (!this.validLiqMaterial(firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord + 1, firstBlockZCoord, world)) {
                              canGenxxxxxxx = true;
                              break;
                           }
                        } else {
                           countBlock2++;
                        }
                     }

                     if (var500 > 20 || countBlock2 > 20) {
                        break;
                     }
                  }

                  if (canGenxxxxxxx) {
                     this.generateOnlyClay(false, firstBlockXCoord, firstBlockYCoord, firstBlockZCoord, (int)Math.floor(radxxxxxx * 1.5F), world, random);
                     TacktGen = true;
                     PXC = firstBlockXCoord;
                     PYC = firstBlockYCoord + 2;
                     PZC = firstBlockZCoord;
                     if (PAC == 0) {
                        genCountxxxx = 40 + random.nextInt(10);
                        PAC = 1;
                     }

                     blockGen = true;
                  }
               }

               float rg = world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 360.0F;
               float imp = 2.0F + world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 1.0F;
               float impV = 7.0F + world.field_73012_v.nextFloat() * 5.0F;
               rg = (float)Math.toRadians(rg);
               XCV = PXC + (int)Math.floor(Math.sin(rg) * impV);
               YCV = PYC;
               ZCV = PZC + (int)Math.floor(Math.cos(rg) * impV);
               XC = PXC + (int)Math.floor(Math.sin(rg) * imp);
               YC = PYC;
               ZC = PZC + (int)Math.floor(Math.cos(rg) * imp);
            }
         }
      }
   }
}
